> Cikakken bayani ga kowane canjin yanayi wanda OmniRoute ya gane shi. Don samfurin farawa mai sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kowane maɓalli da aka rubuta anan kuma dole ne ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma kowane m a cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync` yana tilasta wannan akan aikatawa kuma a cikin CI. Don ƙetare m da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa lissafin izini a cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Asirin da ake bukata

Alamar sirrin da ba a bayyana ba** Ee** an ƙirƙira su ta atomatik a farkon gudu (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) kuma sun dage zuwa `server.env` a cikin kundin bayanan - saita su a sarari lokacin da wannan kundin ya kasance mai ƙarewa. Banda shi ne `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a dagewa zuwa `server.env` - wuraren shigarwar uwar garken yana sanya ƙimar ephemeral kowane-boot kawai gadar da ke cikin tsari za ta iya amfani da ita, don haka abokan ciniki na gada na waje suna buƙatar saita shi a sarari.

| Mai canzawa                  | Da ake bukata    | Tsohuwar                 | Fayil na asali                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**           | _(babu)_                 | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Signs/verifies duk kukis ɗin zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙiri tare da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**           | _(babu)_                 | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Maɓallin ɓoyayyen AES don ƙimar maɓalli na API a sauran a cikin SQLite. Ƙirƙiri tare da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a              | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar wucewa ta dashboard a farawa yayin da babu wanda aka ajiye tukuna; watsi da zarar an ajiye kalmar sirri. npm da tushen suna shigar da kwafin `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, don haka suna farawa da sanannen wurin sa `CHANGEME`: maye gurbin shi kafin fara boot, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (ba komai) a cikin wannan `.env` don ƙirƙirar kalmar sirri a cikin maye na kan jirgin a ziyarar farko, azaman ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohon hoton Docker ) yayi. Canja kalmar sirri da aka adana a cikin Dashboard → Saituna → Tsaro, ko gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin wurin duba tushe) tare da saita `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin bayanan uwar garken. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (sarrafa) | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don na ciki Codex Responses WebSocket gada. Yana tabbatar da buƙatun gada tsakanin relay Electron/browser WS da OmniRoute. ⚠️**REQUIRED a samarwa - lokacin da ba a saita ba, duk buƙatun gadar WS ana ƙi.** Samar da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a              | _(git SHA)_              | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Bayyanannen ma'aikacin sabis na cache-busting id don harsashi na PWA (#11779); na farko a cikin sarkar ƙuduri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a              | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a cikin sarkar - wanda masu ginin PaaS saita (misali salon Heroku) kamar yadda aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a              | _(an samu)_              | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a na ginin lokaci abokin ciniki yana amfani da shi don yin rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; wanda aka samo daga biyun sama, sannan git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (atomatik)   | _(atomatik kowane taya)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin-tsari da ke tabbatar da amintaccen tambarin abokin-IP ya fito daga uwar garken HTTP na OmniRoute(`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Amintattun authz middleware suna buƙatar yanki (loopback/LAN gating na hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY ) kawai lokacin da tambarin ya ɗauki wannan alamar. An ƙirƙira ta atomatik kowane taya - bar ba a saita ba; kawai saka shi don saitin tsari da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba tambarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a              | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | Wuraren waƙafi IPs ko CIDR na waƙafi waɗanda `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` lissafin IP allow/deny na iya yin imani, a saman madauki, adiresoshin hanyar sadarwa masu zaman kansu da gefuna Cloudflare, waɗanda koyaushe ana dogara. Ana buƙatar kawai don wakili akan kowane adireshin jama'a; in ba haka ba wannan adireshin na wakili shine abin da tace ta yanke hukunci. Maɓallan maɓallan kowane abokin ciniki (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) na relay akan adireshin abokin ciniki ɗaya da aka warware, ba akan tura masu kai kaɗai ba.                                                                                                                                                               |

### Umarnin ƙarni

```bash
# Generate all four secrets at once:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kar a taɓa yin fayilolin `.env` tare da ainihin sirrin sarrafa sigar. `.gitignore` ya riga ya keɓe `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Adana & Database

OmniRoute yana amfani**SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) domin duk dagewa. Waɗannan masu canji suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayen rayuwa.

| Mai canzawa                                | Tsohuwar                      | Fayil na asali                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tushen directory don SQLite DB, madadin, da fayilolin bayanai. Shake don kundin Docker ko hanyoyin da aka saba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Tserewa ƙyanƙyashe don mai gadin test/eval DATA_DIR (#10428). Gwaje-gwaje da Node eval/print bincike (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gami da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=` ) ba tare da `DATA_DIR` ana tura su zuwa madaidaicin jifa ba don haka ba za su iya buɗe ainihin bayanan mai aiki ba; saita zuwa `1` don komawa zuwa ainihin kundin adireshi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na kayan aikin gudu. An buga tambarin `npm run build:release`; allura a cikin kwantena waɗanda ke jigilar su ba tare da sakon `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Fitacce kamar `system.buildSha` akan `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ƙofofin fakitin kayan tarihi yana bincika ginin SHA da (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar shirya ginin wanda SHA baya kan layin sakin, yin rikodin shi azaman canary da gangan maimakon gazawar ƙofar (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | maɓallin API don binciken binciken hayaki na canary, wanda aka aika azaman `Authorization: Bearer` akan `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun turawa kawai ke amfani dashi (#10429), ba ta uwar garken ba. Ba shi da alaƙa da masu canjin `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na zaɓin shiga CLI kayan hayaki (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) - duba [CLI Integrations → Tsararriyar hayaki na gaske](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Siginar Gina-lokaci (#10060): saita zuwa `1` ta `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` kuma kowane ma'aikacin gini ya gaji don haka layin DB ya dawo da stub mara-op maimakon loda ɗan ƙasa mafi kyawun-sqlite3 addon (wanda ke zubar da ma'aikaci yayin fita). Kar a taɓa saita uwar garken da ke gudana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake bincike na zaɓi na zaɓi na ɗan ƙasa na zaɓi don bishiyar da aka sayar. Wannan baya sanya abin dogaro da ya ɓace ya zama mai ginawa; yi amfani da shi kawai lokacin da aka ba da abubuwan dogaro na asali ba tare da bandeji ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Fallback mai suna** don `DATA_DIR`, duba kawai lokacin da `DATA_DIR` bai saita ba. An yi amfani da shi don nemo ma'ajiyar ma'ajiyar zare mai mannewa akan faifan mai aiwatar da PromptQL (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita var ba, cache ɗin yana tsayawa a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya kawai (ba a ci gaba da farawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Directory da** Runtime plugin scanner** yana karantawa - kuma tushen mai sarrafa plugin ɗin ya shigar da shi - yana kawar da tsohowar gida (#11827). Nuna shi a bishiyar plugin ɗin da aka ɗaure a cikin Docker/K8s maimakon motsa HOME kawai don ƙaura hanyar binciken (HOME yana sarrafa kowane ɗabi'ar dangi ma). Cire = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` lokacin da tsarin ke fitar da gida ba kwata-kwata - rashin gano shirun wannan canjin yana cirewa. Ana shigar da adireshin da aka warware sau ɗaya a farawa azaman `scanner.dir_resolved` tare da shigarwar da ta yi nasara. Gefen uwar garken kawai: CLI plugins umarni suna kiyaye nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(Ba a kashe = ba)_           | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoye bayanan SQLite a sauran. Ƙirƙiri tare da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar don maɓallin ɓoyewa. Haɓakawa lokacin yin jujjuyawar maɓalli don tallafawa ɓarna tsoffin ma'ajin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Lokacin da `true`, ya tsallake routine/pre-write SQLite madadin fayiloli (models.dev farashin save/clear, saituna ya rubuta). Manual da pre-dawowa madadin har yanzu suna aiki. Yana yi** ba** musaki hoton ƙaura mai dorewa mai ɗorewa ko mai gadin ƙaura don DB mai dorewa. Abubuwan ajiyar da ba na hannu ba ana matsawa zuwa aƙalla sau ɗaya a cikin mintuna 60. Dashboard** Saituna → Ma'aji** zai iya kashe madadin atomatik na yau da kullun da kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(unset/off)_                 | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Milliseconds; dabi'u da ke ƙasa 1 ko waɗanda ba na ƙididdigewa ba sun tsaya a kashe; yayi kashedin sau ɗaya a kowace 10s; Layin log ɗin yana da direba, aiki, da wucewaMs kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** za `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Karɓi azaman koma baya lokacin da farkon farkon ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** (Siffa mai ƙididdigewa na Base64) karɓa azaman koma baya. Ƙaddamarwa ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Soke tazarar gwajin lafiyar SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Lokacin da ba a saita ba, ana samun ɓarna daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` mai raye-raye na lokaci-lokaci na iya ɓata taswirar wal-index da aka raba tare da rushe tsarin tare da SIGBUS (#13973), don haka babu mai tsarawa. Madaidaicin ba shi da aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana yin rikodin gargadin yanke hukunci na lokaci ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ke yin shiru. Ana kiyaye WAL ta wuraren bincike PASSIVE (a ƙasa) kuma an yanke shi ta wurin binciken rufewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Rage tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` akai-akai (ms). Yana ci gaba da jiran firam ɗin WAL ƙanana don haka wuraren bincike su tsaya da sauri kuma fayil ɗin WAL yana tsayawa a ɗaure tsakanin guntun rufewa. `0` ya kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Lokacin da alamar PASSIVE ta sami fayil ɗin WAL sama da wannan girman, gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` don haka WAL ya fara aiki ba tare da sake rubuta taswirar wal-index ba. An cire wuraren bincike kai tsaye-yanayin (duba jeren `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS` ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don fita aikin bayan an ci gaba da matsin lamba mai mahimmanci don `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, barin mai kulawa (tsarin `Restart=always`, Docker sake farawa manufofin) ya dawo da tsari mai tsabta maimakon bautar 503s har abada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Har yaushe matsatsi mai mahimmanci dole ne ya ci gaba kafin sake farawa da kai wuta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don yin watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` mai fa'ida (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) lokacin da maƙwabci mai hayaniya yayi tafiya 503s yayin da wannan tsari yayi kyau (#13124). Cgroup da V8 masu gadin tulin suna tsayawa. PSI kuma yana dakatar da kirgawa azaman hujjar magudanar kwaya don `cgroup_high`: jimlar rukuni sama da `memory.high` sannan ta zama mai mahimmanci kawai akan karuwar `memory.events` `high`/`max`. Ba tare da `memory.events` ana kiyaye danyen rabo ba.                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Yana aiki ne kawai da zarar an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED` ; yayi daidai da taga ɗimbin fitarwa na OpenAI na Batch API. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` mai aiki da mai aiki, wanda ke tsayawa ba tare da sharadi ba (babu tacewa) ta ƙira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Lokacin da `true`, bari tsaftacewar atomatik ta share ayyukan Batch API na ƙarshe (da wuraren binciken su) da suka wuce `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, kuma a share abun cikin BLOB na fayilolin da aka ɗorawa da suka wuce `expires_at` nasu. Kashe ta tsohuwa: kowane shigarwar da ke akwai yana adana wannan bayanan daidai kamar yadda ya gabata har sai mai aiki ya shiga. | `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta madaidaicin duba lafiyar DB, koda lokacin da yawanci za'a tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka na ɗan gajeren lokaci).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake ɗumi-ɗumi na lokacin gudu yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani lokacin jigilar jigilar ƙaura a cikin gine-gine na al'ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundayen adireshi na ƙaura kamar yadda shigarwar `namespace=dir` ke raba ta hanyar hanyar dandamali (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Fayilolin da aka samo a wurin ana yin rikodin su azaman `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarraba jigilar jigilar nasa ba zai taɓa yin karo da ramummuka na sama ba. Shigar da ba ta da tsari, wurin suna mara inganci ko bayanan da ya ɓace yana jefawa a farawa maimakon yin shiru da tsallake tsarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Matsakaicin aminci na ƙaura da yawa (#3416). Farawa yana zubar da ciki idan fiye da wannan ƙaura da yawa suna jiran a kan wani DB da ke wanzu (masu tsaro a kan tebur mai gogewa). Tada shi don maido da tsofaffin madadin; saita zuwa `0` don kashe cak.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Jagorar aiki don ƙofar sakin `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan GB 12 ( ~ 3 GB biyu suna shigar da bishiyoyi tare da kwalta), don haka ba dole ba ne ta gudana akan ƙaramin tmpfs - akan mai gudu mai ɗaukar nauyi `/tmp` yana da 12 GB RAM mai goyan bayan tmpfs kuma ƙofar ya ƙare, yana yanke kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(default in code)_           | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazara tazara (ms) don marubucin spend/cost da aka daidaita. Ƙananan dabi'u suna rage haɗakar rubutu; manyan dabi'u suna rage jayayyar DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(default in code)_           | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin shigarwar abubuwan da aka kashe kafin turawar tilastawa. Haɓaka kan manyan ayyukan QPS; ƙananan lokacin da ƙayyadaddun ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya yana da mahimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da rajistan ayyukan gyara kuskuren `[ProxyFetch]` akan hanyar relay Vercel. Unset yana rubuta `<path>.generated` kusa da fayil ɗin da aka ɗauka kawai. Yanayin mai watsa shiri kawai: ba a taɓa adana shi a cikin bayanan ba ko karɓa ta hanyar gudanarwa API, saboda ana aiwatar da binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Yadda ainihin wakili na gida ke sake lodawa bayan an tabbatar da maye gurbin saitin: tsarin gardama JSON yana gudana ba tare da harsashi ba, ko daidai `external` lokacin da mai duba waje ya yi amfani da fayil ɗin. Ba a taɓa adanawa a cikin bayanan bayanai ko fallasa ta hanyar API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗawa client/egress IPs da prefix asusu a cikin fi'ili `[ProxyEgress]` layin tsari-log. An kiyaye OFF ta tsohuwa don kada log ɗin tsari ya ƙyale IPs ko prefix na asusun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga binciken binciken lokaci na kowane-buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin keɓaɓɓen CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(moto)_                      | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Bayyanar hanya ta bincika lafiyar kwantena. Ba a saita ba, binciken ya samo shi daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita shi ya koma cikin zurfin sa ido karshen.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima mara amfani don fitar da binciken `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin cache harsashi CLI (read/refresh/write). Kashe ta tsohuwa - waɗancan cache ɗin sun gaza shiru don haka ma'aunin missing/corrupt ba zai taɓa karya cikawar shafin ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin sake gwadawa taga don daidaitattun abubuwa (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokacin an yi alama sun kasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin tushe (ms) don juzu'i na juzu'i akan sake fasalin abu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Tafi (ms) don juzu'i mai ma'ana tsakanin matakan sake gwadawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batches da aka sarrafa lokaci guda. Tada don ƙara yawan kayan aiki; kiyaye ƙasa don guje wa guguwa mai iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin canza bayanan dagewar data kasance, mai gudun hijira yana buga cikakkiyar hoto mai bayani da abun ciki a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Bugawa yana buƙatar tsarin fayil wanda ke goyan bayan tsarin fayil iri ɗaya, ba tare da sake rubuta hanyoyin haɗin yanar gizo ba tare da aiki tare na fayil mai dorewa. rundunan POSIX kuma suna buƙatar daidaita tsarin gudanarwa; a kan Windows, Node na iya ƙin hannun adireshi, don haka OmniRoute ya watsar da fayil ɗin da aka buga kuma yana ɗaukar daidaitawar shigarwar directory azaman mafi kyawun ƙoƙari. Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka ɗora ba zai iya ba da waɗannan garantin ba, farawa ya kasa rufe kafin amfani da ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa ƙarar tare da waɗancan abubuwan farko; kar a yi amfani da `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don ketare amincin ƙaura.

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi                | Kanfigareshan                                                                    |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaban gida**           | Bar duk abubuwan da ba a so. DB tana zaune a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.        |
| **Docker**                  | `DATA_DIR=/data` + ya hau ƙarar a `/data`.                                       |
| **Rufaffen lokacin hutawa** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + kiyaye ajiyar maɓalli! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Testing**              | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` - ephemeral, babu boye-boye da ake bukata.        |

---

## 3. Network & Ports

| Mai canzawa                                 | Tsohuwar                     | Fayil na asali                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                      | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar jiragen ruwa na duka Dashboard UI da API (yanayin tashar tashar guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(Ba komai tushen = )_       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | URL subpath for serving OmniRoute behind a reverse proxy (sets Next.js `basePath`; auth redirects are basePath-aware). E.g. `/omniroute`. In Docker the value is baked during `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); pre-built root images can apply a different runtime value once at container start before Next.js boots. Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` to the public origin including the same subpath.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(Ba komai tushen = )_       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Madubin mai lilo na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`, wanda aka lika a lokacin ginawa don haka nunin ƙarshen dashboard yana nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Faɗuwa zuwa `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` lokacin da ba a saita ba. Sake ginawa bayan canzawa ( `basePath` na gaba shine lokacin ginawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(Ba za'a iya saita = )_     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Ci gaba da shigar iframe na shafukan HTML. Ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana jigilar `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` don hidimar shafukan (dashboard, login, docs, landing) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma babu `X-Frame-Options`, don haka lambar VS Mai Sauƙaƙan Browser na iya sanya su (yanayin OmniCopilot's `dashboardOpen: "editor"` ). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabin matakin tushen) yana kiyaye tsattsauran rubutun ko wace hanya. `vscode` kawai aka gane — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Gina-lokaci: sake ginawa bayan canzawa (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi akan shigarwa da aka riga aka gina ba shi da wani tasiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Lokacin da aka saita, yana hidimar `/v1/*` proxy API (da Gemini-native `/v1beta/*`) akan wannan keɓantaccen tashar jiragen ruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Daure adireshin tashar tashar API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Lokacin da aka saita, yana hidimar Dashboard UI akan wannan keɓantaccen tashar jiragen ruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_             | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Haɗin tattaunawa a cikin jirgin sama lokaci guda; buƙatun sama da hula samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Integer mai kyau yana ba da damar gadi; unset/`0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman don wannan ƙofa lokacin ɗaure misalin OmniRoute. Yana ba da damar dubawar madauki mai shigowa. Haruffa masu izini: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; matsakaicin haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | OmniRoute tushe mai URLs wanda zai iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`.
