| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                       | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` a `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`            | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don musaki sabar WebSocket ainihi (wanda aka kunna ta tsohuwa, mai ɗaure madauki). jujjuya CI/harness wanda ke hana rubutun mataimaki WebSocket kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                         | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Per- (alama, IP) iyakar saurin gudu, requests/minute. A cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya, kowane misali. `0` ko korau yana kashe ƙofar girman IP (kowane-alamar DB iyaka har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                 | Next.js core                                                             | Yana sarrafa maganganun shiga, caching, fallasa dalla-dalla kuskure, da inganta Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — code tsoho) | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shine tsoho mai tarawa don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (2-3 × da sauri ginawa, alamar alama). Saita zuwa `0` don komawa cikin fakitin gidan yanar gizo akan Windows, lokacin da yake gudana cikin rashin daidaituwa na ɗaurin ɗauri / haɗakarwa,** ko akan na'urori masu takurawa RAM** samar da — Turbopack yana ginawa akan wannan layin sigar Next.js (16.2.x) an san su sama zuwa kololuwa mafi girma a ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya fiye da fakitin gidan yanar gizo akan manyan jadawali (Madaidaicin 16.3's Turbopack ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya na gaba bai tsaya tsayin daka ba tukuna); fakitin gidan yanar gizo ya yi ƙasa sosai. Duba #6409.** Hoton Docker yana gina tsoho zuwa `0` (pack web)** : `Dockerfile` ya furta `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` don haka bare `docker build .` (Layin Railway da sauran rundunonin dannawa ɗaya, ad-hoc kai hosters) baya samun OOM-kashe akan maginin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya; wuce `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don gina hoton da Turbopack. Dubi albarkatun lokacin Gina `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` →. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Samar da fakitin gidan yanar gizo yana ginawa (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) tsallake fakitin gidan yanar gizon ma'ajin tsarin fayil na dindindin sai dai idan wannan shine `1`. Rubuta wannan cache yana ƙara kusan 2.5 GB na ƙwaƙwalwar ƙira mafi girma a 6144 MB heap cap, kuma tsaftataccen gini baya karantawa. Saita zuwa `1` don kiyaye `.build/next/cache/webpack` don saurin maimaita ginawa akan injin tare da RAM don adanawa; Docker yana ginawa ɗaukar shi azaman `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (matakan maginin `Dockerfile` da `Dockerfile.bun` ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake gwajin amincin SQLite akan farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin boot akan manyan bayanan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | systemd (sd_notify yarjejeniya)                                          | Saita ta systemd lokacin da tsari ke gudana ƙarƙashin sashin sabis tare da haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Mai amfani bai taɓa saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_            | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Nau'in = notify / WatchdogSec=) koda lokacin yana gudana ƙarƙashin naúrar tsarin. Mai sanar da shi babu-op a waje tsarin ko da kuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                    | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar (ms) don mai tsara tsarin tantance lafiyar bayanan bayanan. Mafi qarancin: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                     | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) don yanayin kiwon lafiya da aka adana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                      | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin baya-bayan nan na haɗin yanar gizo. Masu samar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe ana cire su daga share — "tabbatar" su "tabbatarsu" ita ce ainihin tambayar da aka biya ta sama, don haka ba a taɓa bincikar lafiyar su akan mai ƙidayar lokaci (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                          | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar ingantaccen mai kira ya nemi `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke kwatanta saman da aka kammala sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Masu kiran da ba a san sunansu ba ba su taɓa kunna binciken ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                       | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Matsakaicin tsayawa (ms) don rikodin madauki na taron-madauki: daskarewa fiye da wannan rajistan ayyukan layin `event loop stalled` guda ɗaya a ci gaba tare da ƙididdigewa da ƙididdigewa cikin jirgin, kuma yana cin karo da `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum akan `/api/monitoring/health`. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa; ƙimar da ba za a iya karantawa ba ko mara kyau tana komawa zuwa `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                    | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Daure adireshin uwar garken Next.js dev/start. Yana soke tsohuwar `0.0.0.0` lokacin da aka saita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin ɗaure da mai gudu Playwright ke amfani dashi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Matsalolin zuwa `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwajen hermetic.** Kada ku yi amfani da `omniroute serve`** — yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` maimakon (POSIX harsashi auto saita `HOSTNAME` zuwa sunan injin; `.env` ba zai iya soke shi ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                    | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Daure adireshin `omniroute serve`. Yana guje wa karo tare da madaidaicin POSIX harsashi `HOSTNAME` (ko da yaushe ana saita zuwa sunan injin ta bash/zsh). Faɗuwa zuwa `0.0.0.0` lokacin da ba a saita ba. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Hanyoyin Port

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tabbatarwa

| Mai canzawa                                 | Tsohuwar                                                                               | Fayil na asali                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                  | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Gishiri haɗe tare da abubuwan gano kayan aiki don buga yatsa na inji. Canza kowane turawa don keɓewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(cire = bazuwar kowane shigar gishiri ya ci gaba a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | gishiri HMAC don samo alamar tabbatarwa ta CLI na gida. Saita wannan ƙimar tana jujjuya duk alamun CLI akan injin kuma koyaushe yana ɗaukar fifiko akan gishirin da aka dage. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita tutar `Secure` akan kukis ɗin zama.** Dole ne ya zama `true`** lokacin gudu a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                | API middleware                                           | Lokacin `true`, duk buƙatun wakili `/v1/*` dole ne su haɗa da maɓalli API inganci.** Wannan tuta ba ta kofa `GET /v1/models`** , wanda ke biye da yanayin shiga dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) maimakon - don haka `401` daga `/v1/models` yana nufin NOT yana nufin an kiyaye shi. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar bayyana cikakkun ƙimar maɓalli API a cikin Dashboard UI. Ƙaddamarwa daga Tutocin Siffofin Dashboard; hadarin tsaro a kan abubuwan da aka raba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(Ba komai)_                                                                           | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | Maɓallin API mai waƙafi IDs waƙafi wanda ke keɓance buƙatun shiga (cikon GDPR ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(unset = unlimited)_                                                                  | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin kuɗin buƙatun yau da kullun da aka yi amfani da shi ga maɓallan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ba shi da amfani. Unsetting ko fanko: babu fayyace hula (#2289, #11017). `0` iri ɗaya ne (mara iyaka). Integer N yana ba da damar N/day, 5N/week, 20N/month. Maras kyaun dabi'u marasa fanko sun koma ga gadon 1000/day, 5000/week, 20000/month windows.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Matsakaicin izinin buƙatun girman jiki. Ya ƙi biyan kuɗin da ya wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙungiyoyin neman na ainihi a ko sama da wannan bakin kofa suna ɗaukar tsarin izinin atomatik-na gida kafin yin la'akari da JSON (hanyar BYTE, gami da `POST /v1/responses`). Guda guda [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) lafiya-gidan gudun hijira kamar tsari-mai nauyi; har yanzu yana da iyaka da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) don haka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) baya dawowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙaƙƙarfan hular hanyar taɗi da aka tilasta wa karatun bytes lokacin da aka iyakance, gami da buƙatun da suka ɓace, mara inganci, ko `Content-Length` marar gaskiya; wuce gona da iri yana karbar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(Ba a saita ba - babu buƙatar ƙidaya)_                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-farin:** wannan bukata ta gado-COUNT hula yanzu tana ɗaure ne kawai lokacin da aka saita a bayyane. Hagu ba a saita shi ba (tsoho), shigar da hira mai nauyi a maimakon `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` ne ke shigar da shi - kasafin kudin BYTE da aka samu ta atomatik wanda aka ƙima daga ainihin rufin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya a cikin tsarin.** tsari daya** (guda daya V8). ~ 750k-alama mai haɗe-haɗe `/v1/responses` zubar da ciki ~ 12 Gi heaps (#7849) - a** ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya-kasafin kuɗi** gargadi, ba babban samfurin max na 2. Tsarin lafiya (tuba a ƙasa da rabon zubar) MAY shigar da ƙarin lokaci mai tsawo `/v1/responses` via `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Dubun dogayen abokan ciniki na SSE (40-50) suna da yawa + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba "max 2". Makantar da haɓaka wannan don "amfani da mai watsa shiri" ya sake ƙaddamar da #7849. Yawan ninka** tsibi** tare da** N mai zaman kansa `DATA_DIR` s** (#11024); taba `replicas>1` akan fayil SQLite guda daya.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(wanda aka samo ta atomatik)_                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-farin:** ƙetare don kasafin kuɗaɗen byte ɗin da aka samu ta atomatik (25% na madaidaicin silin ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup wanda aka raba ta 8x haɓakawa na wucin gadi). Ƙimar da aka samo da bayyane ta manne zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Jiki mafi girma fiye da kasafin kuɗi mai tasiri ya gaza nan da nan tare da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; gardama tsakanin gawawwakin da za a iya yi wa hidima ɗaya ɗaya ya kasance wanda za a iya sake gwadawa `503`. Abokan ciniki na SSE 40-50 na lokaci ɗaya shine wannan tsibin + na kasafin kuɗi, ba mai wahala "max 2". Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Matsakaicin zubar da matsa lamba (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don shigar da BYTE da STRUCTURE nauyi (#10183, #10268, #10437). Buƙatar nauyi mai nauyi da ta wuce `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` ana zubar da ita kawai tare da `503` mai sake gwadawa lokacin da tulin ya kasance ALSO a ko sama da wannan rabo; A kan lafiyayyen tsibi ana shigar da shi ta ɗakin ɗakin lafiya maimakon.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (default `1`)                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarfafa ƙarin ƙarfin don hanya mai sauri mai lafiya (#10437) akan** duka biyu** STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, gami da jikin ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan daure ba, duk buƙatun buƙatun aiki-amma-koshin lafiya ya ƙetare shiga ba tare da rufi ba. Da zarar wannan hayaniyar da yawa na lokaci guda suna aiki ta hanyar lafiya-tari, ƙarin buƙatun buƙatun sun faɗo zuwa hanyar SAME bounded-wait/shed da ake amfani da su a ƙarƙashin matsi na gaske. `0` yana kashe hanyar wucewa gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiddigan saƙo wanda ke rarraba buƙatar taɗi a matsayin nauyi koda lokacin da jikinsa ke ƙasa da madaidaicin byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙididdiga kayan aiki wanda ke rarraba buƙatar taɗi azaman nauyi mai nauyi koda lokacin da jikinsa ke ƙasa da madaidaicin byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙimar girman kirtani mai ra'ayin mazan jiya wanda ke rarraba buƙata azaman nauyi; wannan wakili ne na kudin shiga, ba alamar cajin mai bayarwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (nakasassu)                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Takaitaccen tarihin ficewa na zaɓi. An kashe ta ta tsohuwa: ƙidayar saƙo shine manufar turawa, ba mallakin buƙatu na duniya ba, kuma yin taɗi a nan ya ƙi tattaunawa da tashar tashar `413` kafin bututun matsawa ya iya sa su iya aiki. Girman tsibi yana da iyaka da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da zubar da matsa lamba. Saita ƙima mai kyau akan ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya waɗanda ke buƙatar rufi mai wuya; wuce gona da iri sannan ya sami ingantaccen tsarin da ake buƙata `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                     | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Ƙaƙƙarfan hula don amsawar da ba ta gudana ba tana cike da cikakke cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya. Bayan wannan an soke mai karantawa na sama kuma buƙatun ya gaza da sauri maimakon girma igiya mara iyaka har sai tarin ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                  | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin bytes na waya da aka tura daga saman kai martani. Lokacin da aka ƙetare kasafin kuɗi, ana jefar da manyan kantuna masu mahimmanci (misali, `x-codex-*` na al'ada, `x-oai-request-id`) don kasancewa cikin iyakoki na gama-gari na baya. Saita mafi girma don tura ƙarin metadata na sama akan farashi mafi girman girman martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                                      | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Legacy asalin tushen CORS izini. Fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` don sababbin turawa. CORS kawai don abokan cinikin API na asali ne kawai; ingantaccen dashboard ya rubuta amfani da buƙatun asali iri ɗaya tare da kariyar CSRF mai ɗaure zaman maimakon.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                                      | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izini na CORS. Ba a aika da kati sai dai idan an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a sarari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Hatch-kawai kuɓuta ƙyanƙyashe don amsa kowane mai bincike `Origin`. Kar a kunna abubuwan da aka raba ko samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                 | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Legacy alias. Ana karanta jujjuyawar dashboard ɗin sa (a DB override) kafin wannan darajar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a cikin ko dai ya juya mai watsa shiri ya duba yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yake yi, ko da a kashe dashboard ɗin tuta; sauran dabi'u suna barin mai gadi zuwa tutoci biyu da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe duban runduna mai gadin URL mai fita, toshe metadata na girgije ya haɗa (duk na 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), akan hanyoyin bada sabis waɗanda ke bin yanayin gadi: ingantaccen URL, gano ƙirar ƙira, tushen mai bada-node URLs (gami da nodes rerank na nesa), da gwajin dawo da wakili, a tsakanin sauran masu ba da image/media mai nisa. Hakanan yana ba da damar maƙasudin mahaɗar gidan yanar gizon masu zaman kansu, ban da wuraren ƙarshen cloud-metadata/link-local, waɗanda ke tsayawa a toshe ko da tare da wannan tuta a kunne. Buƙatun taɗi da aka aika ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da bincika bayanan metadata. Masu samar da gida suna aiki tare da ɓangarorin: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsohuwar `true`) sun riga sun ba da damar gida da LAN URLs akan ingantattun hanyoyin, ganowa, da hanyoyin samar-node; gwajin wakili-fallback da makasudin gidan yanar gizo masu zaman kansu suna tuntubar wannan tuta kawai kuma su toshe rundunonin local/LAN yayin da yake kashewa. Ya soke `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Ƙimar da aka ajiye daga dashboard toggle tana gaba da wannan canji. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                 | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Bada izinin mai bada URLs akan adiresoshin local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, kewayon masu zaman kansu) don tabbatar da mai badawa, gano samfuri, taɗi, da sauran hanyoyin fita waje mai bayarwa da ke kusa da yanayin tsaro.** Tsohuwar `true`** (na gida-farko): mai gadi sannan ya toshe wuraren ƙarshen girgije-metadata - duk 169.254.0.0/16 tare da sanannun sunayen mahaɗan metadata (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita `false` don yanayin jama'a kawai, wanda ke toshe masu zaman kansu da kuma madauki na madauki. Mai gadi yana duba sunan mai masauki ko IP a zahiri kamar yadda aka rubuta, kafin duk wani binciken DNS ; a cikin yanayin jama'a-kawai abin nade lafiya-debo shima yana warware sunan mai masaukin kuma ya ƙi kowace amsa ta sirri. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan cak a kan hanyoyin jerin jerin sahu. Gina-cikin masu samar da gida, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, tsallake duk masu binciken maɓalli don ingantaccen maɓalli da hira. Ƙimar da aka ajiye daga dashboard toggle tana gaba da wannan canji. (#5066) |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Bari hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (rubutu, magana, fassarorin) su yi amfani da kullin mai dacewa na OpenAI wanda aka shirya a wajen localhost. Kashe ta tsohuwa - sarrafa sauti zuwa mai watsa shiri mai nisa yana canza ainihin egress kuma dole ne ya zama takamaiman yanke shawara mai aiki. Loopback/private nodes (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba da izini koyaushe kuma ba a shafa su ba. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Bari `POST /v1/rerank` (da madaidaicin injin madaidaicin matakin) yin amfani da kumburin mai ba da jituwa mai jituwa na OpenAI wanda aka shirya a waje da localhost - akwatin LAN ko Tailscale peer yana gudana TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. Kashe ta hanyar tsohuwa - hanyar turawa zuwa mai watsa shiri mai nisa yana canza asalin egress kuma dole ne ya zama yanke shawara mai aiki. Nodes na Loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba da izini koyaushe kuma ba a shafa su ba. Tushen kullin nesa URL dole ne kuma ya wuce manufofin URL na mai bayarwa (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(Ba komai)_                                                                           | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen mai watsa shiri (waƙafi ko farar sararin samaniya) waɗanda ke ba da hanya-kumburi suna ɗaukar azaman na gida - yawanci sunan sabis na Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko sautin gefen sauti akan hanyar sadarwar OmniRoute ta Docker (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), don haka kumburin baya buƙatar akwati mai lamba IP. Mazaunan da aka jera sun cancanci `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da duba lafiyar gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane kumburi mai nisa ba, kuma ana kiran su kai tsaye - ba ta hanyar `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko wakili na haɗin gwiwa ba. Har yanzu suna aika maɓallin API na haɗin haɗin. Sunaye guda ɗaya kawai da sunayen `.internal`/`.local` ana karɓa; jama'a FQDNs, IP na zahiri da kuma girgije-metadata sunayen ana watsi da su tare da gargadi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, musaki shigar da kalmar wucewa ta yadda masu amfani za su iya tantancewa ta hanyar OIDC Single Single. An kuma yarda da wanda aka fi sani da `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` ; Tutar fasalin Dashboard na maɓalli ɗaya yana ɗaukar fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Bare alias of `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Lissafin Taurara

```bash
# Production security minimum:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Requires HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Authenticate all proxy calls
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Never expose keys in UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # 5 MB limit
```

---

## 5. Sanitization & PII Kariya

OmniRoute tana ba da tsaro mai Layer biyu: na'urar dubawa ta gefen buƙatun da tsiri-gefen PII.

> **Iyakance ⚠️ :** Waɗannan hanyoyin tsaro sune abubuwan gano _mafi kyawun ƙoƙarin heuristic_, ba cikakken tsarin tacewar allura da sauri ba ko tsarin PII DLP. Za su iya samar da ingantattun abubuwan karya (mai kyau persona/RPG ya sa tuta) da kuma rashin kuskure (leetspeak, tazara, tsarin da ba na Ingilishi ba). Ba su isa su kaɗai ba don biyan bukata. Kunna hanyoyin kuma gwada zirga-zirgar zirga-zirgar ku kafin dogaro da su.

### Bangaran Buƙatu: Mai gadin allura da sauri

| Mai canzawa                       | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna duba saƙonnin masu shigowa don tsarin alluran gaggawa.                                                                                                                                              |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufar allura: log ɗin `warn` = kawai, `block` = ƙin yarda da buƙata tare da 400. Legacy `redact` yayi** ba** rubutun allurar tsiri; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` don buƙatun PII sake rubutawa. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy alias ga `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — hali iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin tsananin da `MODE=block` ya ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Matsakaici alamu ana lura da su-kawai sai an saukar da su.                                                              |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy alias ga `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — hali iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                        |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Lokacin da `true`, sake kunna PII a cikin buƙatun masu shigowa (wanda ba shi da yanayin allura).                                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Sake sanannun maɓallan API-maɓalli / alamar-asiri daga abubuwan biya na request/response. Ficewa; madubi `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                                         |

### Response-Side: PII Sanitizer

| Mai canzawa                      | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali            | Bayani                                                              |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika amsoshin LLM don leaked PII kafin komawa ga abokin ciniki.  |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = mask PII, `warn` = log kawai, `block` = sauke duka amsa. |

### VS Code Tokenized-Hanyar Sanitizer Context

| Mai canzawa                         | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`      | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yanke mahallin madaidaicin aiki-edita (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana gyara abun ciki na fayiloli masu mahimmanci a haɗe. Amintaccen-ta-tsohuwar; saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. |

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi           | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Yarda da kasuwanci** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (tubalan allura + request/response PII redaction; hanyoyin suna zaman kansu) |
| **Saka idanu kawai**   | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — rajistan ayyukan amma ba ya toshewa                                                                                                         |
| **Amfani na sirri**    | Bar duk nakasassu - sifili sama da sama                                                                                                                                                                   |
