## 6. Ka'idojin Kayan aiki & Hanyar Hanya

| Mai canzawa                                                 | Tsohuwar                        | Fayil na asali                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                      | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Yana sarrafa shiga LLM tool/function-calling. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka lissafa kawai, `denylist` = duk sai an jera su, `disabled` = babu hani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`    | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Hanyar da per-model/protocol JSON tweaks).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                          | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Sake loda tazara (ms) don zazzafar zazzage fayil ɗin ƙa'idodin biya. Mafi qarancin `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                         | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Shiga: hanyar da babu `claude-*` samfurin IDs daga abokan cinikin lambar Claude ta cikin asusun Claude Code OAuth maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai bayarwa. Bayyanar bayanan prefixes na masu bayarwa har yanzu suna nasara. Hakanan ana iya daidaita shi ta hanyar jujjuyawar dashboard akan shafin mai bada Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                          | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Saita `false` don dakatar da OmniRoute ƙara `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` Anthropic beta kanta (bantattun sa hannun sa hannu kawai). Buƙatun tare da `thinking.display: "summarized"` tsallake shi ko da kuwa; beta-tattaunawar abokin ciniki ko zaɓin shiga `requestDefaults.redactThinking` mai dacewa da CC yana kiyaye shi. buƙatun Claude OAuth suna kiyaye beta (yana daga cikin lambar Claude OAuth yatsan hannu) sai dai idan an kunna fasalin fasalin `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` na tsoho.                                     |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                         | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Legacy `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` hali (#4825): maye gurbin kowane sunan ƙira tare da zaɓin haɗin `defaultModel`. Kashe ta tsohuwa, kawai `auto` ko samfurin fanko ne aka maye gurbinsu; yi rikodin gargadi ɗaya kowane biyu idan kun kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                             | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Kyawun-samfurin madaidaicin madaidaicin gwanjo-robin combos (#9100). Semaphore na zagaye-robin ya kasance mai wuyar gaske a buƙatun lokaci guda 3 akan kowane ƙira ba tare da jujjuyawa ba, yana jera manyan zirga-zirgar ababen hawa a bayan wannan hular. An tabbatar da `>= 1`, manne zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                         | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Shiga cikin haɗari mai haɗari wanda ya tsallake OmniRoute's mahallin mahallin gida-taga / max-input-token duba don buƙatun samfuri ɗaya kai tsaye. Masu samarwa na sama har yanzu suna aiwatar da ainihin iyakokin su; matsawa da sauri da hular alamar fitarwa ta samfurin ta ci gaba da aiki. Ingantacciyar fifiko shine Tutoci Siffar DB sun ƙetare yanayin > m > tsoho; babu buƙatar sake farawa.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Takardun YAML `providers:` na layi (RIC-738, D4). Lokacin da aka saita (tare da ko ba tare da toshe `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa shigarwar OpenAI mai dacewa da kai mai ɗaukar nauyi maimakon bututun girgije. Unset (tsofaffin): hanyar ta faɗo kai tsaye zuwa bututun girgije. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML mai riƙe da takaddun `providers:` iri ɗaya kamar `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don turawa waɗanda suka fi son fayil akan env var na layi. Ko wanne yana kunna shigar da kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita - buɗaɗɗen hanya)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Maɓallin API da aka raba na zaɓi don haɗin kai mai ɗaukar nauyi (D5 scaffold, wanda aka tanada don tsarin keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen maɓalli). Lokacin saita, buƙatun dole ne su haɗa da `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Ba a saita ba: hanyar a buɗe take, ta yi daidai da tsarin mai bayarwa na gida mai ɗaukar nauyin kai (loopback/trusted-network deployment).                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Takaddun YAML `strategy:` na linzamin kwamfuta don ingin mai ƙayyadaddun tuƙi (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, mai warwarewar sanyi, fifikon farashi, rashin jinkiri, sarkar koma baya. Yana soke shingen `strategy:` na layi wanda aka yi shi cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML mai riƙe da takaddun `strategy:` iri ɗaya kamar `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don turawa waɗanda suka fi son fayil akan env var na layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Saita `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a kula dashi ba kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya fayyace tarihin saƙo, kuma ana rufe zaman IDs wanda abokin ciniki ya kawo shi ma. Gudanar da zaure ba ya canzawa kuma ba a share bayanan da ke akwai. Don turawa waɗanda basa amfani da kallon zance na dashboard kuma suna son tebirin ya daina girma.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | maɓallin API don zaɓin shiga `nadir` auto-combo na'ura mai ba da hanya tsakanin hanyoyin sadarwa, ana amfani da shi lokacin da haɗin `config.nadir.apiKey` ɗin kansa bai saita ba. Na zaɓi: kira mara waya zuwa ƙasa akan matakin da ba a san sunansa na Nadir ba, wanda ke da iyaka ga kowane IP don haka ba za a iya amfani da shi ba a bayan ƙofar da aka raba. M; gwammace filin hada-hada lokacin da combos ke amfani da asusu daban-daban.                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`      | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Tushen tushen shawarar Nadir API, ana amfani da shi lokacin da ba a saita `config.nadir.baseUrl` ɗin kansa ba. Ana buƙata kawai don Nadir mai ɗaukar nauyin kansa, wanda ke kiyaye kai tsaye a cikin hanyar sadarwar ku. An jure madaidaicin `/v1` kuma an cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                      | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Tagan mako-mako don iyakar amfani da USD na kowane-API (buƙatar tilastawa, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: satin ya ƙare a farkon sake saitin mako-mako a cikin hanyoyin haɗin da maɓallin ke iya kaiwa, ko kuma kwanaki 7 ne da ba a san kowa ba. `calendar`: mako na gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin mai zuwa 00:00, daidai da kowane maɓalli. ana ba da shawarar `calendar` lokacin da aka raba tafki ɗaya na asusu na sama ta maɓallan kasafin kuɗi da yawa daban-daban. Ƙimomin mara inganci sun koma `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(lokacin aiki)_                | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | yankin lokaci IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke bayyana Litinin 00:00 don taga `calendar` na mako-mako. Unset ko mara aiki: yankin lokaci na tsarin OmniRoute ( `TZ` na mai watsa shiri ko kwantena, wanda galibi ba a saita shi, watau Coordinated Universal Time, a cikin hotunan akwati). An yi watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                           | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Tsohuwar iyakar adadin kayan aikin da aka yarda a cikin buƙata ( tsoho OpenAI ). Lambobi masu inganci kawai; mara inganci ko mara inganci suna kiyaye `128`. Mai badawa tare da iyakar known/proactive (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana amfani da nasa hula - wannan kawai yana motsa silin da aka saba. Maƙasudin tsarin Claude ba su da tsoho (Saƙonnin API ba su da ko ɗaya); iyakar koya har yanzu tana aiki.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(na)_                          | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | SYNTX.ai da sauri-emulated kayan aiki kira: unset ko `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` cusa katalogin kayan aiki na gida cikin hanzari; kowace ƙima (misali `0`) ta tsallake wannan allura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Shake don relays / gwajin kayan aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa bayan wakili na baya (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) lokacin da OAuth sake kiran waya ko ƙirƙirar hanyoyin haɗin jama'a dole ne suyi amfani da sunan mai masaukin. Idan ba tare da wannan ba, sake kiran OAuth na iya yin kasala saboda redirect_uri ba zai daidaita ba kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira na iya nunawa asalin kwantena na ciki.
>
> Riƙe `BASE_URL` azaman loopback/container URL na ciki don ayyukan uwar garke-zuwa uwar garke. Kar a yi amfani da `Origin` mai bincike ko sunan mai masaukin jama'a don ɗaukar bayanan kai na ciki.
>
> Ingantattun dashboard ya rubuta baya buƙatar tsayayyen tushen jama'a URL: dashboard ɗin yana aika buƙatun marasa aminci na asali iri ɗaya tare da alamar CSRF mai ɗaure. OmniRoute har yanzu yana tsakiyar tabbatar da asalin jama'a don haɗin haɗin yanar gizon da ba na dashboard ba: fayyace URL env vars na jama'a ana amincewa da farko; raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` heads ba a yi watsi da su sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma ana buga tambarin wakilin wakilin nan take kamar yadda aka amince. Kar a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara buƙatun dashboard na asali iri ɗaya; CORS don abokan ciniki na tushen-tsaye ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakili mai fita

Hanyar hanyar zuwa LLM mai bada kira ta hanyar HTTP ko SOCKS5 proxy don sarrafa egress, geo-routing, ko IP masking.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` ya sabawa `true`. Saita shi zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` don tilasta HTTP/1.1 akan masu aiko da Undici a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: kai tsaye egress, HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxies, relays, da sabo-sabo socket sake. Wannan yana adana tabbaci na takardar shedar TLS, tantancewar wakili, iyakokin haɗin gwiwa, da saitunan rayayyu. Sake kunna OmniRoute bayan an canza shi saboda ana adana masu aikawa kuma an ƙirƙiri wakilan relay a farawa. Maɓalli baya sarrafa wreq TLS buga yatsa ko takamaiman jigilar mai bayarwa.

| Mai canzawa                              | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 don kira na gaba. Fita tare da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Abokin ciniki-bangaren                         | Wayar da kan abokin ciniki game da samuwar SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tuta ta tsohuwa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override yayi nasara). Wuraren wakili da jujjuyawar kowane-asusu suna dakatar da yin hidima ga memba wanda kawai ya gaza (ƙi binciken TCP, ko 429 ta hanyarsa) na tsawon lokaci wanda ya ninka akan kowane maimaitawa, har zuwa tafiya. `false` yana dawo da zaɓe a sarari.                                                          |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Lissafin ba da izini don maƙasudin zaɓin sarrafawa na gida-core fiye da madauki (comma/space ware runduna ko IPs, `*` yana buɗe LAN/private amma bai taɓa metadata/link-local ba). An ba da izinin dawo da baya akan http/https ta tsohuwa. Ƙimar dashboard DB tana samun nasara akan wannan canjin lokacin da aka saita kuma ba ta da komai.                                                                    |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin tushe (ms) na 429 saiti, wanda aka ninka akan kowane maimaitawa. Karanta sau ɗaya a farawa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ko kuma wanda ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho tare da faɗakarwa.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Cap (ms) na 429 saiti. Karanta sau ɗaya a farawa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ko kuma wanda ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho tare da faɗakarwa. Lokacin da hular ke ƙasa da tushe, ya faɗi baya zuwa tsoho. Rage hula kuma yana rage yawan mantuwa: shigarwar tana share `2 x max` bayan ƙarewa.                                                                             |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasalin ficewa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override wins). Ga masu samar da adadin kuɗin da adireshin egress ya cika, sanya matsayi na memba wanda ke raba adireshin ficewar memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan kusa da mambobi masu lafiya. Oda kawai, ba a cire ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke haifar da siginar ƙi da yake karantawa.                  |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Maimaita tagar (ms) tana maimaita faɗakarwar `proxy.set_aside` webhook kowane memba na tafkin don haka fashewar ƙi ya aika taron guda ɗaya. Karanta akan kowane fitarwa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ba ko kuma ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho a shiru.                                                                                                                    |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | HTTP proxy don kira na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | HTTPS proxy don kira na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | Wakilin duniya (yana goyan bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Sanya makasudin echo-IP wanda masu binciken egress ke amfani da shi zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Ba a saita ba, binciken yana gwada `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org` don haka ba a ba da rahoton mutuwar ramukan IPv4-kawai ba (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | Waƙaƙƙen hostnames/IPs don ketare wakili.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Matsakaicin soket na lokaci ɗaya a kowane cache HTTP/SOCKS wakili mai aikawa. Rafukan SSE masu tsayi kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya lokacin da buƙatun da yawa ke raba wakili na matakin asusu ɗaya. Ƙimar da ke sama da `256` an lissafta su.                                                                                                                                       |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 musafaha (haɗa) lokacin ƙarewa a cikin ms. Tada shi lokacin da babban gidan ƙofa guda ɗaya ya sami babban haɗin kai (misali buƙatun lokaci guda 100) - musafaha na gaske zai iya wuce 10s a ƙarƙashin cikakken tafkin ko da yake ana iya isa ga wakili, wanda in ba haka ba yana saman a matsayin `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` na ƙarya. An buga `120000`.                                       |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Lokacin da `false` (tsoho), buƙatun da aka sanya wakili ya kasa warwarewa shine** ƙi ( gaza-rufe)** maimakon komawa baya zuwa haɗin kai tsaye - yana hana leaks na ainihi-IP. Saita `true` don maido da koma baya DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Spoof TLS yatsa ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Matsalolin JA3/JA4 suna tarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin ba da izini na mai ba da waƙafi don sabon proxied TLS routing (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS yana kiyaye halayen gado lokacin da ba a saita shi ba; kawai waɗannan masu samar da hanya ta hanyar gadar yatsa Chrome-124. Ana cire Groq (`api.groq.com`) koda lokacin da lissafin izinin ba a saita ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana haifar da Cloudflare 1010 yayin da undici na asali ya kai API. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Ba da izinin mahallin mai lilo na Claude Turnstile Playwright don yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi                                       | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta ramin SSH**                            | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                 |
| **Wakilin HTTP na kamfani**                        | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`       |
| **Anti sawun yatsa**                               | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` - yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa)                                                                |
| **Sarrafa Egress / babu damar kai tsaye**          | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (default). Buƙatun sun kasa cika wuya lokacin da babu wakili maimakon yawo ta kai tsaye.            |
| **Legacy / dev - ba da damar koma baya kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da dabi'ar taurin kai: haɗin kai tsaye da ake amfani dashi lokacin da ƙudurin wakili ya gaza. |

> **Bayanan kula (NVIDIA ingantacciyar hanyar wucewa - #3226):** NVIDIA's API-key ingantacciyar madaidaicin wurin tsayawa lokacin da aka ci nasara ta hanyar proxy/TLS-patched ta duniya (undic dispatcher → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan ya ketare facin wakili na waccan ingantaccen kiran ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Wannan rarrabuwa ce, keɓantacce - yana aikatawa** ba** shafi chat/usage egress. Kewayon wucewa yana da iyaka ta hanyar `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗin Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da ƙaddamar da motocin gefe CLI (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Mai canzawa                       | Tsohuwar                         | Fayil na asali                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = tsarin bincike PATH; `manual` = yi amfani da bayyane hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don bincike na binary na CLI (waɗanda ke da mazauni).                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Soke kundin adireshin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya zama cikakke kuma a cikin tsarin gida -** ko** , a cikin akwati, hanya mai ɗaure (haka `/host-home` ke aiki). Wani abu kuma ya koma dir gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Bada OmniRoute don rubuta fayilolin daidaitawar CLI (sakewar alamar, bayanan zaman). Saita zuwa `false` don sanya kowane tsarin tsarin CLI ya kasa rubutawa tare da bayyanannen kuskure "rubuta naƙasasshe".                            |

### CLI Profile Auto-Sync

Waɗannan tutocin fasalin sun fice kuma an kashe su. Hakanan ana iya kunna su daga dashboard na Code CLI.

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan aiki tare da samfurin mai bayarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin adireshi. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; baya taɓa canza saitin active/default Codex, auth, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai bayarwa.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaitawar ƙirar mai ba da sabis, ta atomatik ta sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin martaba na lambar `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude daga kundin adireshi. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; baya taɓa canza saitin active/default Claude, auth, ko zaɓin mai bayarwa. |

### Docker

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana aiki ne kawai lokacin da aka ɗaure hanyar daga mai watsa shiri - haɗa shi tare da hawa kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba bayanin martabar `host` a `docker-compose.yml`). Hanyar da ba ta cikin gidan mai amfani da kwantena ba kuma ba ta da ɗaure ba, saboda za a yi watsi da rubutu a wurin lokacin da aka sake ƙirƙirar akwati.

Hoton yana gudana azaman `USER node`, don haka `/root` wanda ba a saka shi ba shine** ba** mai inganci.

| Mai canzawa                              | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(moto)_          | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano akwati akan (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙata kawai akan lokutan aiki kawai ganowar atomatik ya ɓace.                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Bada damar saitin kayan aiki na CLI ya rubuta cikin hanyar kwantena da ba a hawa ba ta wata hanya. Madaidaicin CLI shine `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                              |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | rashin daidaitawa | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | hoton Docker kawai: dashboard** Sabunta Yanzu** ya rubuta buƙatun JSON zuwa wannan fayil (dole ne ya kasance a cikin `DATA_DIR`) don mai kula da mai masaukin baki don ja da sake ƙirƙirar akwati. Duba [Jagorar Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Binary (`omniroute`) mataimaka

Waɗannan masu canji suna daidaita halayen binary na `omniroute` CLI (ba gano motar gefe a sama ba).

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                          | Fayil na asali                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(tsarin)_                        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen fitarwa CLI. BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana soke tsarin gida env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr uwar garken zuwa tasha a yanayin da ake kulawa. Daidai da tutar `--log` akan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Alamar tabbatar da na'ura ta allurar a matsayin kai `x-omniroute-cli-token`. An ƙirƙira ta atomatik a cikin ɗawainiya 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Dagewar Gemini tushen gida; Gudun harbi ɗaya yana amfani da keɓantaccen gida na ɗan lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin alheri a cikin milli seconds kafin tilastawa-kashe yaro mai alamar CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da bayyananniyar DATA_DIR don tsallake tsohuwar fayil ɗin mahalli na bayanan bayanan mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Alamar daidaitawa don `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` yana karɓar alamar daidaitawa kawai, ba maɓallin API ba). Daidai da `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ƙoƙarin ƙarshen HTTP (ms) don buƙatun sabar CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) CLI yana jiran ƙarshen lafiyar uwar garken kafin buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga jinkirin farawa sanyi (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saitawa ta hanyar `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don buga binciken retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Littafin al'ada don gano kayan aikin CLI ( fakitin `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Tsoffin zuwa `~/.omniroute/plugins/` lokacin cirewa. CLI-kawai - ba zai taɓa zuwa na'urar daukar hotan takardu ta gefen uwar garken ba, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nunawa (sashe na 2).                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Danne abubuwan dubawa lokacin da aka saita zuwa kowace ƙima mara fa'ida: sanarwar CLI a wurin fitowar tsari da banner "sabuntawa akwai" dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` sannan ya tsallake npm, GitHub da duban labarai). Hakanan sanarwar CLI tana tsayawa a cikin CI, yanayin shiru, da fitowar mara rubutu.                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(an samu)_                       | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Rayuwa URL bincike ta `omniroute doctor`. Zaɓin `livenessUrl` na shirye-shirye yana ɗaukar fifiko; in ba haka ba ana amfani da wannan darajar da baki. Ba a saita ba, CLI yana samun ƙarshen `/api/health/degradation` ɗin dashboard na gida.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Hanyar kasidar mai bayarwa da umarnin mai bada CLI ke amfani dashi. Zaɓin `catalogPath` na shirye-shirye yana ɗaukar fifiko. Ana amfani da cikakkun hanyoyi kamar yadda yake; hanyoyin zumunta sun warware daga tushen aikin. Ba a saita ba, CLI yana amfani da kundin adireshi na masu badawa, tare da gadon fayil `providers.ts` azaman koma baya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(Tsohon mai bayarwa)_            | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Samfurin faɗuwar duniya don gwajin haɗin mai bada CLI. An saita fifikon ƙirar haɗin kai, ƙayyadaddun ƙirar mai badawa ya soke, wannan ƙimar, sannan ƙirar gwajin da aka gina a cikin mai bayarwa.                                                                                                                                                    |

Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                   | modules MCP/A2A                                             | maɓallin API don kayan aikin MCP na ciki da kiran fasaha A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | Maɓalli na ID don siffanta log ɗin duba MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                   | Gado                                                        | Legacy wanda aka yi wa lakabi da `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Kwanaki na tarihin aikin A2A da aka adana a cikin bayanan gida kafin sharewar yau da kullun ta goge jere. Ba a saita ba, mara lambobi, ko `<= 0` yana komawa zuwa `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-canza don fasalin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiyar A2A -hits abin lura. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken tunowar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya don aiki gaba ɗaya; kowace ƙima (ciki har da unset) tana kiyaye ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana ba da damar wakilcin fitowar offline/local da aka yi rikodi na ƙarshen ƙarshen. An kashe barin sai dai idan yana gudanar da aikin rikodi na gida a bayyane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci (ms) don Gudun Matsalolin Batutuwa guda ɗaya. Manne zuwa iyakar ciki; yana komawa ga ginanniyar tsoho lokacin da ba a saita ko ba daidai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _( mahallin mai aiki)_                              | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | CLI m-yanayin context/profile don umarnin `omniroute` ; ya kawar da mahallin aiki a cikin shagon mahallin gida. Daidai da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe zaɓin `keytar` OS-keychain backend don bayanan mahallin CLI. Lokacin da aka kunna, takaddun shaida suna kasancewa cikin yanayin `config.json` `0600` kuma CLI yana fitar da gargaɗin faɗuwa sau ɗaya; an yi niyya don aikin headless/container da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Ƙaddamar da ikon tushen isa ga iyaka akan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(duk)_                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Wuraren waƙafi: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Kunna ƙimar: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Zubar da kasafin kuɗi (ms) don MCP hops waɗanda ke jiran mai bayarwa (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Manne zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Model katalogin tazara tsakanin sa'o'i.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Iyakan mai bayarwa da tazarar kada kuri'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar (ms) tsakanin zaɓen ƙididdiga OAuth a jere a cikin babban aiki tare; Ana debo hanyoyin haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don gujewa fashe wani sama. `0` ya fita (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Min tazara (ms) tsakanin raƙuman ƙididdiga na sama a jere akan hanyar preflight/monitor ta kowace buƙata; sararin samaniya yana kiran cibiyar sadarwa da yawa asusu da yawa akan IP ɗaya ba sa fashe sama. Waya cikin Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (duka shafukan debo), OpenCode, da Crof quota fetchers (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe babban hanyar aika `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba ana bibiyar — daban. Cache ya buge ba a shafa ba. `0` yana kashe; matsawa `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Ƙarin tazarar bazuwar kowane-buƙata (ms), samfuri a kowane keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen keɓaɓɓen. Defaults zuwa 120; manne `0..60000`; `0` yana cire jitter kawai. Saita `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` don musaki duk ma'aunin. Jitter baya bada garantin hana hana ruwa gudu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Jinkirta (ms) kafin iyakan mai ba da wartsakewa bayan wani taron amfani na gaske, yana ba da lokacin ragi API don yin rijistar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKERS`                      | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Matsakaicin ma'aikatan RTK/Caveman masu aiki tare; wuce gona da iri jira FIFO.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                            | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci-lokaci a cikin milli seconds. Ma'aikatan da suka ƙare sun ƙare kuma buƙatar ta gaza buɗewa ba canzawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Zaman rayuwa marar aiki a cikin milli seconds kafin a ƙare ma'aikacin matsawa mara amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_MAX_OLD_MB`            | `1024`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | V8 tsohon-tsara hep cap a cikin MB ga kowane ma'aikacin matsawa, duk yanayin ( `128` bene). Aikin da ya wuce shi ya kasa buɗewa (ba a haɗa shi ba) maimakon girma tulin ƙofa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | T02 mai jujjuya-bututun kowane injin kewayawa mai jujjuyawar mai sarrafa.** Shiga (an kashe tsoho)** — lokacin da aka kunna, injin da ke jefa akai-akai a kan buƙatun ana tsallake shi ( gaza buɗewa) don sanyi; kashe = byte-daidaicin hali na gado.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | An sami gazawar buƙatun giciye a jere kafin mai fashewar injin ya buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Milliseconds wani buɗaɗɗen injuna ya tsaya tsayin daka kafin binciken rabin buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | T08/H8 CCR maidowa-bayar da martani: kowane kafin dawo da toshewar da aka adana yana ɗaga ingantaccen `minChars` a layi (abin da ake samu akai-akai yana matsawa ƙasa; `>=3` maidowa = bai taɓa matsawa ba). `1` yana hana hawan (tsallewar binary a bakin kofa kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | CCR mai dorewa block store (#9061). Yana ba da ajiyar ajiyar ajiyar ajiya tare da SQLite don haka toshe ya tsira daga korar LRU, TTL, sake farawa, ko dawo da saukowa a wani misali. Saita `false` don ajiye tubalan a ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya kawai. Tubalan sama da 512KB da lokutan gudu na girgije suna zama ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya-kawai ko da kuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | T08/H5 prefix mai daskare babban canji mai amfani da aka lura.** Shiga (an kashe tsoho)** — lokacin da aka kunna, tsarin da aka lura da sauri `>=` ana kula da bakin kofa azaman tsayayyen prefix mai iya adanawa kuma ana kiyaye shi daga matsawa har ma ga masu samarwa abin da ke ɓacewa a tsaye (daskare _preserves_ kawai, ba zai taɓa canzawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Abubuwan lura da tsarin suna faɗakarwa kafin a ɗauke shi azaman daskararren prefix.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Saita `true` ta rubutun bootstrap bayan saitin farko. Sarrafa saitin mayen gani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hatch na tserewa: ba da izini ga ƙungiyar neman ta soke filin aikin Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Google Daya AI manufofin ƙididdigewa: `off` bai taɓa allurar ƙididdigewa ba, `retry` ya yi allura sau ɗaya bayan adadin da ya cancanta 429, kuma `always` ya yi allurar akan buƙatun farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_MAX_429_ATTEMPTS`                  | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/antigravity429Guard.ts`                  | Upstream 429s da aka jure a kowane Antigravity (`agy`/`antigravity`) buƙatu a duk runduna (1-12) kafin 429 su koma ga mai kira. Jikin da ya kashe shi ana amsawa a cikin gida don sake aikawa (minti 2, ninka zuwa 30 min, ko alamar sake gwadawa na sama), kuma mai sarrafa taɗi baya sake fara buƙatar bayan wannan 429.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Ba da izinin fassarar buƙatun Antigravity ya tsallake tabbataccen sa hannun CLI ɗin sa lokacin da babban rafi ya ƙi sa hannu na gaske (yanayin debug/antiquated-CLI ). Mara sifili yana ba da damar wucewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Mayar da hanyar fayil ɗin alama Antigravity CLI (agy) don gano shigowar gida ta atomatik.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### CLI OAuth Na ciki)

| Mai canzawa         | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                  | Bayani                                                |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-ganewa       | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Sabar uwar garken URL CLI↔OmniRoute auth gada.        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Alamar tabbaci don gadar CLI.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID mai amfani don zaman gadan CLI.                    |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Legacy wanda aka yi wa lakabi da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.  |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Legacy wanda aka yi wa lakabi da `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.   |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Legacy wanda aka yi wa lakabi da `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`. |
