## 11. OAuth Takardun Mai bayarwa

Gina-in takardun shaida don** ci gaban localhost** . Don ƙaddamar da aiki mai nisa, yi rijistar naku a kowane na'ura mai haɓakawa na mai samarwa.

| Mai canzawa                                 | Mai bayarwa             | Bayanan kula                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Abokin ciniki na jama'a - babu wani sirri da ake buƙata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Shake turawa URI. Default: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Jama'a abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Yana buƙatar daidaita `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Jama'a abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Musa Code (Meta)        | Haɓaka zaɓi na jama'a Muse CLI id ɗin abokin ciniki na kwarara na'urar. Bar unset don amfani da gasa a cikin jama'a abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Yana buƙatar daidaita `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Jama'a abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Haɓaka zaɓi na GitHub Enterprise Copilot's OAuth id abokin ciniki. Faɗuwa zuwa ga tsohon jama'a na `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` lokacin da ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Canjin zaɓi na zaɓi na haɗin kai Copilot abokin ciniki GitHub ID aka aika a cikin `Copilot-Integration-Id` da masu kai `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Tsoffin zuwa `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | faɗuwar maɓalli API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani dashi lokacin da babu shaidar haɗin kai ɗaya. Na zaɓi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Hanyar al'ada zuwa binary Devin CLI (`devin`). An warware ta `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | OAuth abokin ciniki ID don GitLab Duo. Yi rijistar app a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da tura URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Faɗuwa zuwa `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | OAuth sirrin abokin ciniki don GitLab Duo. Na zaɓi - kwararar PKCE baya buƙatar sirri. Komawa zuwa `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Soke tushen GitLab URL ( GitLab mai sarrafa kansa). Tsoffin zuwa `https://gitlab.com`. Faɗuwa zuwa `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Legacy fallback for `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani dashi lokacin da ba'a saita bambance-bambancen `_DUO_` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Gada koma baya ga `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` cinye ta `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Gada koma baya ga `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` cinye ta `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Saita don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Maɓallin maɓalli na API kai tsaye (wucewa OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | Wurin aiki ID don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Alias ga `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Share Qoder CLI config dir (zamanin PAT keɓe, yana guje wa shigar da mai bincike).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                   | Matsakaicin matakan qodercli na lokaci ɗaya (buguwar bugu ko tsokanar ACP ); karin buƙatun layi FIFO. Unset (tsoho) = babu hula.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                   | Har yaushe nema Qoder ke jiran ramin qodercli (kawai tare da saitin hula) kafin kasawa da 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. Default `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                   | `print` (tsoho) spawns `qodercli --print` kowace bukata; `acp` yana kiyaye tsari guda ɗaya `qodercli --acp` a kowane PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                   | safarar ACP : kashe tsarin qodercli na ci gaba bayan wannan lokacin rashin aiki. Default `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                   | safarar ACP : ranar ƙarshe don qodercli don amsa ACP `initialize`. Tsohuwar `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                   | safarar ACP : sake sarrafa tsarin qodercli na dindindin bayan wannan zaman da yawa. Default `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Alamar `tk` ta gaba don aikawa azaman `validated` akan `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatar lokacin da Blackbox ya tilasta madaidaicin alamar; in ba haka ba OmniRoute ya koma zuwa ga bazuwar UUID. Duba fitowar #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge guardrail | BudeAI-mai jituwa tushen URL don kiran gada mara hangen nesa na Anthropic. Tsoffin abubuwan gadar OpenAI URL env ko api.openai.com. Nuna madauki na OmniRoute's `/v1` kai ko kowane madaidaicin buɗaɗɗen buɗaɗɗen AI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Mas'ala ta #2232. Lokacin da URL ya zama OmniRoute na `/v1`, ƙaramin buƙatun yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma ya inganta tare da ƙudurin madauki na kai (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a cikin yanayin gida, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) don haka `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` aikin aika aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail | maɓallin API don URL na sama. Yana soke kowane mai bayarwa OpenAI / Google env vars don kiran gadar hangen nesa ba na ɗan adam ba. Samfuran Anthropic suna kiyaye hanyar maɓalli Anthropic. Mas'ala ta #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail | Yaya tsawon lokacin da "babu ɗan takarar da za a iya amfani da shi" yana ɓoye sakamakon karkatar, a cikin ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko mara kyau sun koma ga tsoho; `0` yana kashe ma'ajin mara kyau. Source: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Takaddun shaida](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials) 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'in: "Aikace-aikacen Yanar Gizo") 3. Ƙara URL uwar garken ku azaman izni da turawa URI 4. Sauya ƙididdiga masu daraja a cikin `.env`.
