## 12. Wakilin Mai Amfani-Aiki Ya Hana

Soke taken `User-Agent` da aka aika zuwa kowane mai samarwa. Ana warware wannan da ƙarfi a lokacin aiki ta rukunin tushe mai zartarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Source:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mai canzawa | Default Value | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin kayan aiki na ɓangare na uku a zahiri zuwa Anthropic akan duka hanyoyin ɗaure ɗan adam ( OAuth na ƙasa da CLIPROxyAPI). Ta hanyar tsohuwa mai zartarwa yana ƙididdige sunayen kayan aikin da ba Claude-Code ba (Claude Code taswirar taswirar inda akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase) kuma ya juya su akan amsa ta hanyar `_toolNameMap`, don haka ba a ƙi yin amfani da kayan aikin maciji kamar yadda abokan ciniki na ɓangare na uku suka buga. Gyara kurakurai kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ke sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Soke sigar abokin ciniki Codex ba tare da cikakken zaren UA ba |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Haɓaka `x-grok-client-version` don ƙididdigewa, ƙirar ƙirar aiki, da lambar na'ura OAuth, da tsohowar Grok CLI Mai amfani-Agent don ƙaddamarwa da daidaitawa samfuri. Yana karɓar SemVer tare da metadata na zaɓin prerelease/build, har zuwa haruffa 128 bayan datsa kewayen sararin samaniya. Cire, fanko, mara inganci, ko ƙimar da ke ɗauke da karya layi suna amfani da fil ɗin da aka aika. Cikakken bayanin mai amfani-Agent mai amfani yana riƙe da fifiko. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Soke sigar lambar lambar Claude da aka tallata ba tare da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic yana ba da wasu samfura akan wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Soke kari mai haruffa 3 OmniRoute da ke rataye zuwa `cc_version=` a cikin tsarin lissafin Claude. Kumburi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` - liƙa sigar kawai tana tallata nau'in `version.revision` guda biyu babu ainihin abin da ke fitarwa. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Soke sigar Copilot CLI da aka tallata ba tare da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin sabunta Antigravity IDE |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin sabunta Kiro IDE |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Soke Kiro social device-code `clientId` (jama'a id) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Ficewa: cikakken saƙon kowane-firam ingantacciyar CRC akan rafin taron Kiro (gyara gurbatattun rafukan) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD da Kiro credit; yana canza kiredit `meteringEvent` zuwa ainihin farashin buƙatu |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Rasa martanin Kiro wanda ke ba da rahoton rashin kiredit maimakon yin rikodin shi ba a biya shi ba |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin sabunta Qoder CLI |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ta sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Kuna iya ƙara wakiltan mai amfani don** kowane** mai bada ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Mai zartarwa yana gina sunan env var a hankali.

---

## 13. CLI Daidaituwar Sawun yatsa

Lokacin da aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake yin odar kanun HTTP da filayen jikin JSON don dacewa da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yin alamar asusu yayin kiyaye IP wakilin ku.

**Source:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Mai bayarwa

| Mai canzawa              | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                      |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Mimics Codex CLI buƙatun sa hannu           |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Mimics Claude Code sa hannun neman sa hannu |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Mimics GitHub Copilot buƙatun sa hannu      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Mimics Antigravity buƙatar sa hannu         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Mimics Cursor buƙatar sa hannu              |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Mimics Kimi Coding sa hannun neman sa hannu |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Mimics Kilo Code sa hannu                   |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Mimics Cline buƙatar sa hannu               |

### Duniya

| Mai canzawa      | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna dacewa da hoton yatsa don** duka** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Kimi Coding CLI ainihi ya soke

| Mai canzawa             | Tsohuwar           | Fayil na asali                           | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`           | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Soke sigar Kimi CLI da aka aika yayin kiran OAuth/API.                                |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(wanda aka kama)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Soke na'urar Kimi da aka kama ID da aka yi amfani da ita a cikin taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sokewar Wakilin Mai amfani (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana ɗaukar oda na kai da odar filin jiki, yayin da Wakilin Mai amfani ya soke takamammen takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna duka biyun kai tsaye.

---

## 14. API Mabudin Masu Bayarwa

maɓallan API don masu samarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye.** Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Masu bayarwa → Ƙara maɓallin API.

Saita ta hanyar masu canjin yanayi madadin Docker ko turawa mara kai.

Samfurin da aka gane: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mai canzawa        | Mai bayarwa                                    |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                       |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                     |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader fallback)     |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (alias for `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)          |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) saka + chat fallback |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (alias for `GEMINI_API_KEY`)            |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` a tsaye don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Ruɗi, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a cikin v3.8.0 saboda lokacin gudu ba ya karanta su - waɗannan masu samarwa sun dogara kawai akan Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rufaffen DB. Dubi sashin _Audit: Cire / Matattu Matattu a kasan wannan takaddar don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su a cikin SQLite kuma suna fifiko akan masu canjin yanayi.
>
> **Jina:** saka `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` yi** ba** lissafin gungu env key lokacin da dashboard `jina-ai` (ko shared `jina-reader`) dangane ya wanzu - `getProviderCredentials` ne cika-farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` kawai lokacin da babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Lokutan kira suna danganta faɗuwar env azaman `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Karatu (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) baya hidimar `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (alias `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana amfani da haɗin dashboard `gemini` da farko. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` kawai lokacin da babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Maganganun kira suna danganta faɗuwar env azaman `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Traffic multimodal na asali yana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` akan `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — N OpenAI `input` abubuwa sun zama N vectors.

---

## 15. Saitunan Lokaci

Dukkan dabi'u suna cikin** millise seconds** . Ƙaddamarwa ta tsakiya a cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsawon Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent; unset: 1260000, raised by declared budgets; set: hard cap; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Mai canzawa                                     | Tsohuwar                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar gajeriyar hanya ta duniya - ta ƙetare duk abubuwan da suka dace na `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimlar lokacin buƙatun HTTP don kiran mai bada sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin shuru tsakanin danyen bytes na sama kafin zubar da ciki. Samfuran da suka tsawaita tunani ba safai suke dakatar da > 90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimlar SSE rafi mai aiki; baya sake saiti akan bytes na sama kuma yana zaman kansa daga `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Lokacin da ba a saita ba, tsoho shine mafi girman kowane samfurin `timeoutMs` a cikin wurin yin rajista (1200000, Codex) da magin 60000, da kuma makasudin da ke bayyana kasafin kuɗi mai tsawo (haɗin `timeoutMs`, sannan kowane-samfurin, sannan matakin mai bayarwa, misali GLM's 3000000) yana samun wannan kasafin kuɗi maimakon 60000. Ƙimar da ta dace ita ce babbar maƙasudi ga kowane maƙasudin da aka ayyana kasafin kuɗi ba zai iya ɗagawa ba. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe kowane manufa.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(an kashe)_                                                      | Ko OmniRoute na iya fitar da layin sharhi na `:` SSE (misali bugun bugun `: keepalive` da `x-omniroute-*` tireloli na metadata). An kashe ta hanyar tsohuwa (#10524) tun da tsayayyen abokin ciniki na OpenAI-jituwa JSON.fasa kowane layin SSE kuma ya fadi akan sharhin `:` ; `data:` bugun zuciya ba ya shafa. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don komawa ciki. Ana amfani da `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Ficewa: Haɗa abubuwan Claude `content_block_delta` a jere (nau'in bulogi iri ɗaya da nau'in delta) wanda aka aika zuwa abokan ciniki Claude, suna watsawa aƙalla kowane N ms. Rashin hasara; `signature_delta` da abubuwan kan iyaka ba su taɓa haɗuwa ba. Yana shafi kowane nau'in delta sai dai idan an saita kowane nau'i mai canzawa a ƙasa. Ana amfani da `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Juyin kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Juyin kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Juyin kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Shiga: batch SSE firam ɗin da aka tura a cikin N ms zuwa rubutu ɗaya ga abokin ciniki. Firam na farko da firam ɗin tasha (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) ba su taɓa jinkiri ba; tare da batching a kunne, ITL yana auna gibin batch. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` ya yi amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Aiwatar da masu sa ido na Byte-stall don mayar da martani. `timer` ta sake yin amfani da mai ƙidayar lokaci ɗaya a kowane ɓangarorin sama (wuta dai dai a kasafin kuɗin rumbun ajiya); `interval` yana adana cak guda ɗaya a kowane rafi tare da lokaci `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, don haka rumbun ya yi gobara a cikin lokacin + na kasafin kuɗi. Ana amfani da `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokaci don karɓar taron ba na ping SSE na farko. Yana gadon `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` lokacin da aka saita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin daidaitawar taga shirye-shiryen taron farko don manyan, kayan aiki-nauyi, ko manyan buƙatun yawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Kill-canza ga `/goal` heuristic. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don musaki cikakken ganowa - ba a taɓa ɗaukaka lokacin shirye-shirye da dawo da rafi ta hanyar buƙatun body/headers ba, yana rage ƙarar lokacin ƙarewar abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin shirye-shiryen taron farko don gudanar da wakilin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun tilastawa da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna dawo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don gudanar da wakilin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ƙayyadaddun ficewa na manufa. Wannan zai iya dawo da ADD ne kawai a saman tsohowar mai aiki - ba zai taɓa kawar da ficewar saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan da ba daidai ba `codex.*` SSE (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya OpenAI SDK's `responses.stream()` tare da 502. Default ON (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Ficewa Codex safarar sabar-sabar. WebSocket ƙarshen (`ws://`/`wss://`) na motar gefen `codex app-server` na gida. Lokacin da aka saita tare da alamar, ana tura buƙatun Codex akan JSON-RPC zuwa motar gefe maimakon HTTP Amsoshi API. Hakanan ana iya saita kowane haɗin kai ta hanyar `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` yayi amfani dashi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Alamar capability/bearer ta layi da aka gabatar ga uwar garken app. Haɗin kai ɗaya: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin da ke riƙe alamar iyawar uwar garken (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani dashi lokacin da ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Haɗin kai ɗaya: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Littafin jagorar aikin jujjuyawar uwar garken app yana gudana cikin. Haɗin kai-tsaye: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar yarda ta wuce zuwa juyar uwar garken app (misali `never`, `on-request`). Haɗin kai ɗaya: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar Sandbox ta wuce zuwa juyar uwar garken app (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Lokacin da aka cire mai aiwatarwa ya ɓace zuwa `workspace-write` (taurara; a baya `danger-full-access`). Haɗin kai ɗaya: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amincewa ta atomatik abubuwan yarda da sabar uwar garken (kisa command/file/permission akan mai watsa shiri). Kashe ta tsohuwa - an hana faɗakarwa ta atomatik; Kiran kayan aiki na kayan aiki ba su da tasiri (suna tafiya daban-daban passthrough `item/tool/call` ). Yana yarda da `true`/`1`/`yes`. Haɗin kai ɗaya: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokaci don karɓar taken martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin jiran masu kai martani akan buƙatun yawo (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan hula; mai bayar da sarari `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` yana ɗaukan fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin amsa-farkon jiran (ms) ga kowane ƙoƙarin no- wakili kai tsaye. Ƙayyadaddun lokaci ya sake gwadawa sau ɗaya akan sabon soket; saita `0` don musaki daure da riƙe dabi'un da suka gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Amsa-fara bene (ms) don yunƙurin wakili kai tsaye wanda mai masaukinsa shine private/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), don haka ba a yanke farkon sanyin Ollama ta 30s na sama a sama. Ayyuka a matsayin bene: jira mai tasiri shine mafi girman wannan darajar da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙima mara inganci yana cire bene na gida. Masu masaukin nesa ba su da wani tasiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Rufi (ms) don sabon-socket RETRY yunƙurin bayan ƙaddamar da yunƙurin mayar da martani-lokacin farawa sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai lokacin da mai kira ya riga ya haɗa siginar ƙarshe na kansa (haɗin da aka warware/samfuri/mai ba da /`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` cascade); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin ɗaure kuma ta fara fara wuta a cikin hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan babban koma baya ne mai karimci maimakon madaidaicin hula - ba tare da shi ba, sake gwadawa ya sake amfani da gajeriyar tagar `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` iri ɗaya azaman yunƙurin da aka haɗa da 504'd samfuran tunani na jinkirin-TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda a ƙasa da bene na sama ba; lokacin da mai kira ya ba da sigina na ƙarshe kwata-kwata, sake gwadawa yana kiyaye falon falon baya canzawa. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokaci don karɓar cikakken jikin amsawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | lokacin kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Rike-alive soket ya ƙare mara amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | TLS wakilin sawun yatsa (wreq-js) ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyaka lokaci-zuwa-farko-byte akan wreq-js TLS-sawun yatsa jiki na musamman; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kadai ba zai iya kama jikin da ya tsaya ba tunda ya warware da zarar masu kai suka iso (#12656). Ƙayyadaddun lokaci ya soke mai karanta wreq kuma ya koma zuwa ga mai aika direct/proxy ; `0` yana kashe mai sa ido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai, kuma kawai yayin da alamar fasalin `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` ke kunne (default off): yana iyakance jira na farkon byte na martani mai gudana bayan masu buga kai (#13484). Ana buɗe rafin martani da `response.created`, don haka shiru da ya wuce wannan taga wani rumbu ne: an kwantar da asusun kuma buƙatar ta juya zuwa asusun na gaba sau ɗaya; rumfar ta biyu ta gaza da sauri. `0` yana kashe mai gadi har ma da tuta a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= kashe)                                                     | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai: yana da iyaka jiran jiran masu amsan amsawa a kan kiran amsawa mai gudana lokacin da akwai wani asusu - buƙatar da aka yi layi ta tsaya jira kuma tana ci gaba maimakon riƙe cikakkiyar taga masu taken. Kiran Amsa da aka watsa kawai ake shafa (Rashin amsawa yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru akwai layi, ba tsara ba); gama chatting da kiran da ba'a taba yi ba, sai taga yana gajarta silin da zai fara aiki mai inganci, bai taba tsawaita shi ba. Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara lokacin kunna: `30000`. `0` ya kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai: sau nawa a kowane buƙatun da aka ɗaure da kai na sama na iya matsawa zuwa asusu na gaba. Sauran asusun na ƙarshe koyaushe yana kiyaye cikakken taga masu kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: kiliya buƙatun tare da bugun zuciya bayan maimaita 429s na wucin gadi (ko sabon alamar tafki), sannan a sake kunna ƙafa ɗaya da aka rufe har zuwa asusun jeri 3 a maimakon fitar da dukkan jiragen ruwa (#13924). Kashe ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juyawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai kamar da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai: lokacin da kowane asusun ajiyar sanyi-da ke cikin wurin shakatawa-da-ci gaba da sake kunnawa aka keɓe shi ta hanyar ƙwaƙwalwar wakili-ƙi, yi musu hidima ta wata hanya maimakon sake kunna komai (#14851, biyowa zuwa #14750). Kashe ta tsohuwa: ƙafar sake kunnawa babu komai a cikin wannan yanayin, kamar a baya #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Taɗi mai yawo kawai: lokacin da na farko na sama ya tsaya kafin samar da taron da za a iya amfani da shi, fitar da ƙoƙari na biyu mai iyaka ta hanya guda ta hanyar zagayawa tare da kasafin shirye-shirye iri ɗaya kuma babu hukuncin asusu. Kashe ta tsohuwa: Jikin farko da ya tsaya ya kasa buƙatun ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai: bayan 429 daga mai bada egress-bucketed akan asusun da ba shi da wakili a ƙarƙashin mahallin tafkin yanayi, tambayi wurin haɗin haɗin don wani memba don ƙoƙari na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress iri ɗaya. Umurni, ba a keɓancewa: tafkin da ya ƙare yana kiyaye halin yanzu. Kashe ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juyawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai kamar da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | OpenCode mai zartarwa kawai: ƙetare hanyar alamar tafki-gudu karanta kafin yin parking (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Wani sabon alama yana shakatawa ba tare da sake kirgawa ba; babu ko tsautsayi ya koma kan ma'aunin fashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin hop proxy don buƙatun gadar `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna mai zartarwar gidan yanar gizo na Firecrawl a misali mai ɗaukar nauyin kai ( maɓallin API zaɓin kashe-girgije).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Lokaci-lokacin-buƙata don mai aiwatar da gidan yanar gizo na Firecrawl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Tushen URL na Tavily `/usage` keɓaɓɓen keɓanta (Iyakokin Masu bayarwa); an yi watsi da shi lokacin da ya nuna `api.tavily.com`. Cire saitin yana komawa zuwa tushen haɗin URL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Lokaci don aika kanun labarai ta hanyar gada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Gadar ci gaba da rayuwa mara amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Lokacin kashe danyen soket (0 = an kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin alheri akan SIGTERM/SIGINT kafin ficewar karfi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Komawa da `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` ke amfani da ita lokacin da ba a saita `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Relay rataye dole ne ya gaza kafin lokacin client/agent (~ 30s) don haka masu kira suna ganin takamaiman gazawar gudun ba da sanda maimakon babban lokaci na gaba. An rufe `29000` don haka koyaushe yana farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Kashe ta tsohuwa - Duk da haka akwai ruɗani, kuma jimlar ta shiga cikin amsoshi a matsayin meta-sharhinci ga abokan ciniki. Saita `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don maidowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Cikakken JS mai wuyar ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun lokacin ƙarewar ƙasar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Rarraba wurin binciken Playwright don hira-cookie mai goyon bayan burauza (`browserPool.ts`); saita `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary `obscura` da aka yi amfani da shi azaman injin farko ta wurin mashigin burauza da Cloudflare Playground executor (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); an gano ta atomatik daga tsarin PATH lokacin da ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna Obscura (`http://host:port`) da ke gudana a maimakon zube ɗaya; tsarin ba ya mallaki wannan tsari (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Bayyanar tashar jiragen ruwa don `obscura serve` da aka haifa; Ana zaɓar tashar jiragen ruwa kyauta ta atomatik lokacin da ba a saita (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Haɓaka buƙatun taɗi na kuki na yanar gizo zuwa cikin hanyar mai amfani da mai bincike (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` don kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Tushen URL don gidan yanar gizon Kimi (kimi.ai Connect-RPC na kasa da kasa) mai zartarwa (`kimi-web.ts`); soke kawai don mirror/proxy ƙarshen maki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken wurin taɗi don mai aiwatar da gidan yanar gizon Kimi (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(an gano ta atomatik)_                                           | Hanyar zuwa tsarin Chrome/Edge wanda za'a iya aiwatarwa don shigar da mai binciken mu'amala ta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ya soke kowane-OS auto-ganewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ kadaitaccen fitarwa_                                     | Ƙaddamar da lokacin ginawa don jagorar fitarwa ta tsayayyun da aka cinye ta hanyar haɗin ginin bayan ginin (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); gina kayan aiki, ba lokacin aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Ƙoƙarin haɗin kai na gadar ƙarshen buƙatun da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` lokacin da yake samar da tsohowar ɗab'i). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin haɗaɗɗen haɗin kai, rashin daidaituwa, ko mai badawa ya soke kawai don yin haɗin gwiwa cikin sauri; dabi'un da ke sama da lokacin da aka yi sama na yanzu an keɓe su zuwa lokacin ƙarewar sama.

`comboTimeoutMs` keɓantaccen kasafin kuɗin agogon bangon gabaɗaya ne a cikin maƙasudan gazawar. Bar shi unset ko `0` don ci gaba da jujjuyawar mara iyaka (tsayawa mai tsauri na minti 10 a cikin `comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu yana aiki). Ƙimar da ta dace ta maye gurbin waccan hanyar aminci don haɗawa. Ci gaba `comboTimeoutMs` fiye da `targetTimeoutMs` don haka gazawar har yanzu yana da lokaci bayan an yi niyya ta farko.

### Matsakaicin Mai Sake Wuta

Tunatar da matakin mai ba da wutar lantarki. Tsoffin suna nuna ma'aunin ƙima da aka yi amfani da su tun v3.6 don haɗin + 500.

| Mai canzawa                                                   | Tsohuwar  | Fayil na asali                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin rashin nasara a jere ga masu samar da OAuth kafin mai karya tafiye-tafiye.                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Sake saitin taga (ms) don mai warwarewar OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin gazawar jere don masu samar da maɓallin API.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Sake saitin taga (ms) don mai karya maɓalli na API.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ƙofar gazawar jere don masu samar da gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Sake saitin taga (ms) don mai warwarewar gida.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: gazawa a cikin taga kafin duk mai bada OAuth ya shiga sanyi.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: taga gazawar ƙidaya (ms) don masu samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: sanyi (ms) da zarar an kai madaidaicin mai bada OAuth.                                                                                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | mai bada OAuth yana ƙaruwa bayan wannan buɗaɗɗen zagayowar.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: gazawa a cikin taga kafin duk mai bada maɓallin API ya shiga sanyi.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: taga ƙidayar gazawa (ms) don masu samar da maɓallin API.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai karya matakin mai bayarwa: sanyi (ms) da zarar an kai madaidaicin maɓalli na API.                                                                                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Mai sanyaya maɓalli ɗaya (ms) da ake amfani da shi ta hanyar jujjuyawar maɓallin API-maɓalli da yawa lokacin da 429 na sama ba ya ɗauke da `Retry-After` (#14573). Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko marasa ƙididdiga sun koma ga tsoho. |

| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Anti-flap taga (ms) yana jure wa ɗan gajeren sanyin sanyi kafin a zubar da fil ɗin cache.                                                                                                                                          |

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi                        | Kanfigareshan                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ƙirƙirar code mai tsayi**         | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (15 min)                      |
| **Daure jimlar rafi tsawon rayuwa** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (21 min)               |
| **Saurin gazawa don samarwa API**   | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                       |
| **Samfuran tunani mai tsawo**       | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin chunks) |
