## 16. Shiga

Tsarin shiga yana rubutawa duka stdout da fayilolin log ɗin jujjuya. `src/lib/logEnv.ts` yana karanta duk tsari.

| Mai canzawa                               | Tsohuwar                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin log: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai karantawa ɗan adam) ko `json` (tsararru).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajistan ayyukan don fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Log hanyar fayil (dangi ga tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Mafi girman girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karba: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko baiti na fili.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Kwanaki don ci gaba da jujjuya fayilolin log ɗin aikace-aikacen.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Madaidaicin jujjuyawar fayil ɗin log.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Kwanaki don kiyaye shigarwar log request/call a cikin ma'ajin bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin shigarwar log ɗin kira a cikin buffer na ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin layuka a cikin tebur `call_logs` SQLite kafin datsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_          | Ƙaddamar da cikakken buƙatar shiga ko kashewa, ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 hour)         | Matsakaicin shekaru don shigarwar rajistar buƙatun marayu mai aiki kafin tsaftace ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Matsakaicin cikakkun bayanai masu jiran aiki (tabbatacciyar lamba); kirga hula: share; byte/map caps: saka.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Ajiye ɓangarorin rafi a cikin kayan tarihi na bututu lokacin `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Shiga (`true`) — kashe ta tsohuwa don ajiye diski.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman log log artifact size a cikin KB lokacin `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin layuka a cikin tebur `proxy_logs` SQLite kafin datsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa client/egress IPs da prefixes asusu a cikin rajistan ayyukan wasan bidiyo `[ProxyEgress]`. Rukunin rajista dashboard/database suna riƙe da cikakkun bayanai.                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don yin rikodin `first_chunk_ms` (aika farawa zuwa byte mai amfani na farko) akan layuka na wakili. Yana nannade kowane jikin da ke sama a cikin rafin wucewar malalaci kuma ya daidaita layin da zarar byte ya zo; kashe ta tsohuwa don haka martani ya wuce ba a taɓa shi ba. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 min)            | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin log ɗin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsayin kirtani yana riƙe a cikin kayan tarihin taɗi (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan tsararru da aka riƙe daga wutsiya lokacin da ake yanke lodin log ɗin hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin zurfafawa kafin an yanke lodin log ɗin hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Maɓallai madaidaicin maɓalli a cikin abubuwan da aka biya na log ɗin hira (0 = Unlimited).                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Duk girman jikin request/response (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani a maimakon cikakken clone. Haɓaka wannan idan dogayen tattaunawa masu alaƙa suna nuna majiɓinci maimakon ainihin saƙon da ke cikin dashboard.                                                                             |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Idan gaskiya ne, `serializeArtifactForStorage` tsallake guntun tushen girma. Gyara kuskure kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 17. Inganta ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya

| Mai canzawa                   | Tsohuwar                                                                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _auto_ (karfe bare);**`1024` a cikin hoton Docker**                                                 | **Nasiha** Docker/standalone V8 heap iyaka (MB). Lokacin da ba a saita ba, an daidaita shi da ƙarfi (~ 35% na tsarin RAM, manne zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shine kawai kasa lokacin da jimlar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya ba za a iya karantawa ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** bayyane** an haɗa darajar kamar `--max-old-space-size` da** yayi nasara** sama da tuta tuta mai karo da NODE_OPTIONS (tuta ta ƙarshe ta V8). har yanzu `omniroute serve` ya fi son tulin NODE_OPTIONS data kasance (#5238). Kar a saita duka biyu zuwa lambobi daban-daban - tsarin yana yin rikodin gargadi suna suna duka dabi'u da mai nasara.** Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka daidaitawa baya gudana a wurin.** Wakilin coding `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` da ɗakin kwana na rukuni - duba [jagorar Docker - lokacin gudu RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; kashe lokacin da ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ba) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Cikakkun tsari - fuse RSS (MB) don ƙwaƙwalwar native/external wanda V8 `heapUsed` ba zai iya gani ba; sama da shi sababbin buƙatun samun 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS ba kasafai yake faduwa ba bayan karu, don haka hada shi da `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                                | Matsakaicin cache tsarin shigar da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                    | Matsakaicin jimlar girman cache mai sauri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (5 min)                                                                                    | Shigar cache da sauri TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                               | Matsakaicin zazzabi = 0 martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                    | Matsakaicin girman ma'aunin ma'auni.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                                  | Semantic cache shigarwar TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                                 | Tushen corpus na gida waɗanda ke kiyaye ma'aunin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya a lokaci ɗaya (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: a iyaka an fitar da fihirisar tushen da aka yi amfani da shi kwanan nan kuma a sake gina shi akan tambayarsa ta gaba. Manne zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba ta ƙididdigewa ba ta faɗi baya ga tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                                | Matsakaicin abubuwan da suka faru na rafi na baya-bayan nan a cikin ma'aunin kallon dashboard kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                                                   | Tsawon yanayin mahallin duniya (alamu) ga kowane mai bayarwa wanda ba shi da m `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Lokacin saita, shi** maye gurbin** catalog, dashboard override da ƙimar rajista na waɗannan masu samarwa; lokacin da ba a saita shi, OmniRoute yana amfani da ginanniyar sarkar sa, wanda maƙasudinsa na ƙarshe shine `128000`. Dubi Tsawon [mahallin Mai-bayarwa](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                               | An tanada karin dakin kan layi lokacin da ake bin ka'idojin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Tsawon mahallin mahallin kowane mai bayarwa (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) kuma yana karanta a** kowane mai bayarwa** fom wanda sunansa aka gina daga mai bada ID a lokacin aiki, don haka ba za a iya jera shi a jere a jere a cikin tebur na sama ko a cikin `.env.example`.

**Suna.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, inda `<PROVIDER>` shine mai bada ID babba, tare da kowane hali a wajen `A-Z` / `0-9` wanda aka maye gurbinsa da `_`. Mai bada ID shine na canonical bayan ƙudurin laƙabi (ɓangaren `provider` na zaren `provider/model` sau ɗaya an warware ɗan gajeren laƙabi). Buɗe AI- ko nodes masu jituwa na Anthropic suna amfani da kumburin ID da aka samar, don haka UUID wani ɓangare ne na sunan mai canzawa. Ƙimar ita ce ingantacciyar lamba ta alamomi; wani abu (marai, `0`, korau, mara adadi) an yi watsi da shi kuma ƙuduri ya matsa zuwa mataki na gaba.

| Mai bayarwa ID                                              | Sunan mai canzawa                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| wakili na'ura mai ba da hanya tsakanin hanyoyin sadarwa     | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| mai budewa                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| budeai-mai jituwa-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Gabatarwa.** Don nau'in `provider/model`, mataki na farko da ke samar da ƙima yana samun nasara:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` ga mai bayarwa.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (yanayin duniya ya wuce).
3. Tagar kowane samfuri: mahallin da aka dage yana juyewa (wanda aka saita a cikin dashboard, ko mai daidaitawar ganowa, duba `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), in ba haka ba ƙasidar / model.dev da aka daidaita.
4. rajistar mai bayarwa `defaultContextLength`.
5. Samfurin-sunan heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Gina-in na kowane mai bayarwa, sannan `128000`.

Duk matakan yanayi biyu sun shafi** duka mai bayarwa** : kowane samfurin wannan mai bada yana samun iyaka iri ɗaya, kuma sun yi nasara akan abin da ya wuce kowane samfurin dashboard. Lokacin da samfuri ɗaya kawai na mai badawa yana buƙatar taga daban, yi amfani da jujjuya kowane ƙira a cikin dashboard maimakon.

**Iyakar** Wannan sarkar tana tafiyar da duba mahallin mahallin-taga lokacin buƙatu (ƙirar `context_length_exceeded` / "Input ya wuce mahallin mahallin" ƙi a cikin `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), kasafin matsawa da sauri da iyakokin mahallin mahallin. `context_length` da `GET /v1/models` ke tallata har yanzu yana iya fitowa daga kasida ko metadata na rajista kuma ba shi da tabbacin yin nunin ƙetare muhalli.

**Misali.** Mai bada wanda samfurinsa ya karɓi taga alama 1,050,000 yayin da kasidar ta bayar da rahoton 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Sake kunna uwar garken bayan canza shi: ana karanta darajar daga yanayin tsarin uwar garken. A kan** Desktop app** uwar garken yana gudanar da aikin yaro kuma baya ganin tsarin tsarin da aka saita bayan ƙaddamar da app; sanya layin a cikin fayil ɗin `.env` ɗin aikace-aikacen ya yi lodi (tsarin neman a [Electron Jagora: Duba fayil ɗin muhalli](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), sannan ka daina gabaɗaya kuma sake buɗe app ɗin.

### Matsi

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar          | Bayani                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | rashin daidaitawa | Amintaccen aikin `.rtk/filters.json` ba tare da zanta na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani kawai a cikin ci gaban gida mai sarrafawa. |

### Injin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (shiri na 21)

### Farashin madauki na taron ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya, ƙwarewa, da wartsakewar alama (#10349)

OmniRoute a** Tsarin Node guda ɗaya** . Ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya extraction/retrieval, gwaninta allura, da kuma samar da alamar wartsake suna gudana akan hakan** madauki guda taron** kamar yadda `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba zaren ma'aikaci ba ne.

| Aiki                         | Lambar                             | Tsohuwar                                                | Ikon sarrafawa                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Cire ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya/dawowa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard** An kunna ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya** (default a kan) | Kashe** Saituna → Memory** . Babu keɓantaccen maɓalli na kashe env da ya wuce kashe fasalin a cikin saitunan.             |
| alluran gwaninta             | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard** An kunna basira** (default a kan)           | Kashe** Saituna → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Sandbox knobs a ƙasa kawai daure kisa bayan an riga an kunna allura. |
| Alamar wartsakewa            | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Kunna don masu samar OAuth/web masu alaƙa               | Cire haɗin mai badawa ko bari alamun su kasance masu inganci; babu `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env yau.                             |

Idan `/healthz` yana jinkirin akan akwatin shiru, kashe ƙwarewar ƙwaƙwalwar + da farko, sannan a duba nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasalulluka suna haifar da maki `await` amma har yanzu suna gasa don zaren ɗaya.

Haɗa Layer, ma'ajiyar vector da ƙwanƙwasawa don tsarin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya na dindindin (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mai canzawa                          | Tsohuwar                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (5 min)            | TTL don cache mai shigar da ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (kowace sa hannun source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | An adana mafi yawan shigarwar LRU a cikin ma'ajiyar sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id don fita-in `@huggingface/transformers` bututun MiniLM na gida (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id don madaidaicin tebur potion/Model2Vec mai sakawa. Zazzagewar cikin kasala cikin ma'ajiyar dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Littafin da aka yi amfani da shi don adana fayilolin samfurin potion a tsaye. Matsaloli a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` lokacin da ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Shake tushen Hugging Face Hub tushen URL da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani dashi (misali madubi na ƙarshen saitin saitin iska).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohuwar top-K da `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector search in `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Matsakaicin Matsayi Fusion akai-akai na `k` ga matasan FTS5 + vector maidowa (Recipe-sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seam a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): lokacin da `true`, tilasta ma'ajiyar vector zuwa `null` (yana kwaikwayi yanayin cloud/WASM ba tare da `sqlite-vec`), mai lalata ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya zuwa binciken kalmomin FTS5. Bar rashin saitawa a samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | maɓallin API don Notion backend (wanda aka sani `genericBackend.ts` saitaccen saiti na baya yana amfani dashi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Tushen URL don Notion API (zai iya ƙetare don madadin Notion mai ɗaukar nauyin kansa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | maɓallin API don Obsidian Vault backend (wanda `genericBackend.ts` sanannen saiti na baya ke amfani dashi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Tushen URL don Obsidian Vault API (zai iya ƙetare don vault mai nisa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 buga memori decay master switch.** Shiga (an kashe tsoho)** - sharar gida** yana sharewa** ruɓaɓɓen tunanin. Tare da shi, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` sune na'urori masu tsabta kuma ba a taɓa share komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) bayan haka ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba ta lalace. `0` yana sa rigakafi na episodic shima. Nau'u masu ɗorewa (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) koyaushe suna da rigakafi. Agogon lalacewa ya sake kafawa akan `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar ƙwaƙwalwar da aka yi wa `>=` wannan sau da yawa yakan zama rigakafi ga ruɓe ba tare da la'akari da iri ba. `0` yana hana samun damar rigakafi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (nakasassu)             | Tazara (dakika) don ɓarna na zaɓi na lokaci-lokaci a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ unset = babu share lokaci-lokaci. Shiga shiga sau biyu: kuma yana buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | ID na mai bada waƙafi (wanda ba shi da hankali) wanda ke karɓar saƙon `system`** kawai a index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Don waɗannan, ɓangarorin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya na tsakiyar tsararru ba shi da aminci a cikin tattaunawa mai juyi da yawa, don haka ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya shine merged/prepended azaman jagorar saƙon tsarin maimakon. Tsoffin zuwa `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; Tsawaita ga wuraren ƙarewa masu jituwa na OpenAI mai ɗaukar nauyin kai (misali Qwen3.5 +/3.6) wanda samfurin taɗi wanda ke aiwatar da ƙuntatawar saƙon-jagora guda ɗaya. |

### Low-RAM Docker Misali

`128` dashboard-kawai. Wakilan coding akan wannan tudun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kar a yi amfani da wannan misalin azaman ƙofar Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## 18. Daidaita farashin

Aiki tare da bayanan farashin samfurin atomatik daga kafofin waje.

| Mai canzawa             | Tsohuwar      | Fayil na asali           | Bayani                                    |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Ficewa-a daidaita farashin lokaci-lokaci. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Tazarar daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙa.        |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Tushen bayanan waƙafi.                    |

## Arena ELO Sync

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar      | Fayil na asali                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Lokaci-lokaci Arena AI jagoran allo ELO daidaitawa, ana iya daidaita shi daga Tutoci Feature na Dashboard ko tare da `false` don ficewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Fom ɗin prefix da aka yi amfani da shi don ƙirar ƙira a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana tallata duka gajeriyar laƙabi da prefix na canonical na kowane samfuri (daidaituwar baya - kusan ninki biyu kasida); `alias` yana fitar da id ɗaya a kowane samfurin; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken prefix na mai bada-id (masu samar da wanda aka lakafta su da sunan canonical id suna kiyaye shigarsu guda). Abokan ciniki na iya soke kowane buƙatu tare da `?prefix=alias`. Duba [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Tazarar daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## PromptQL Mai ba da filin wasa (Unofficial/Experimental)

Gadar zaman zaman GraphQL mai juye-juye don gaggawa.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Duk zaɓin zaɓi - abubuwan da ba daidai ba suna nunawa a wuraren ƙarshen filin wasan jama'a; soke kawai don aika self-hosted/alternate PromptQL.

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar                                                           | Fayil na asali                                                          | Bayani                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ƙarshen GraphQL da aka yi amfani da shi don ayyukan chat/session.                |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL ƙarshen abin da aka yi amfani da shi don tambayar kiredit balance/usage. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ƙarshen Ƙarshen da aka yi amfani da shi don mafi kyawun ƙoƙarin-ƙoƙartawa alama. |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) don yin `thread_events` kafin lokacin ƙarewa.           |

---

## Mai Bayar da Yanar HyperAgent (Unofficial/Experimental)

Gadar zaman juyi-injiniya don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Na zaɓi - wuraren tsoho a wurin ƙarshen billing/usage na jama'a; soke kawai don aika self-hosted/alternate HyperAgent.

| Mai canzawa            | Tsohuwar                                            | Fayil na asali                          | Bayani                                                                           |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Ƙarshen Ƙarshen da aka yi amfani da shi don ɗauko tubalan kiredit billing/usage. |

---

## Ƙididdigar Amfani da Code Kilo

Ma'auni USD na sirri da neman amfani da Kilo Pass don mai bada lambar Kilo. Na zaɓi - wuraren tsoho a jama'a Kilo API; ƙetare kawai don daidaitawar relay/test. Tabbatarwa yana amfani da alamar samun damar OAuth na haɗin haɗin.

| Mai canzawa    | Tsohuwar              | Fayil na asali                        | Bayani                                                                                                |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Tushen URL da aka yi amfani da shi don ɗaukar ma'auni na lambar Kilo na sirri da amfani da Kilo Pass. |

---

## Adobe Firefly Web Provider (Unofficial/Experimental)

Wartsakar da zaman mai bincike don mai ba da gidan yanar gizon Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi - duk abubuwan da ba a iya amfani da su an tsara su don shigar da tebur na yau da kullun.

> **An cire a #9255.** An maye gurbin tsohon CDP da aka haɗe Chrome Runtime (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) da sabis na shiga na Playwright, kuma kullinsa ba ya wanzu. ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART masu canji, da ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a cikin codebase - saita su ba shi da wani tasiri.

| Mai canzawa                          | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome mara kai na gaskiya (wanda aka sani-karye don ƙirƙira; cire kuskure kawai).                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | opt-in/out. `0` mai ɗorewa mai bincike yana hana ɗumi mai ɗorewa (murmurewa tsakiyar-tsakiyar 408 har yanzu tana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe ci gaba da zaman Adobe Firefly zuwa faifai.                                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Matsakaicin tazarar (ms) da aka aiwatar tsakanin ƙaddamarwa na gaba, ta tsallake tsohowar da aka gina a ciki.           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazari (ms) da aka ƙara bayan tsari mai nasara, wanda ya wuce tsohuwar ginanniyar ciki.                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkirin tushe (ms) kafin ƙaddamar da buƙatun tsara, ƙetare tsohowar da aka gina a ciki.                                |

## 19. Model Sync (Dev)

| Mai canzawa                         | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ƙarfin jujjuyawar samfura.dev daidaita farashin. Cire saitunan martabar = > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** nasara akan DB** kuma ya tsallake duka biyun lokaci-lokaci sync da `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON scans (farfadowa lokacin da dashboard ɗin ke kwance akan madauki ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` sojojin daidaita kan. Farashin save/clear har yanzu yana kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda babu-op a ƙarƙashin maƙarƙashiya na mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kasida ta lokacin haɓakawa a cikin daƙiƙa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazarar (dakika) don gyara mahallin mahallin-taga mai daidaitawa (5004): fitattun windows-mai ba da sanarwar windows daga gano `/models` kamar yadda `auto:discovery` ke tsallakewa lokacin da suka bambanta daga kasidar. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake yin amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon ɗauko); baya sake rubutawa `manual` ya soke.                                                                                                                                                |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` kawai don relays na ɓangare na uku ne waɗanda ke karɓar abokan ciniki na Code Claude keɓaɓɓen. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta buƙatun don haka waɗannan relays ɗin su karɓi su. Idan kawai kuna son amfani da Claude Code CLI, ko kuma ba ku da tabbacin menene waɗannan relays ɗin, kiyaye wannan naƙasasshen kuma ƙara mai ba da sabis na Anthropic na yau da kullun maimakon.

### Matsakaicin sake dawo da rafi

Halin riƙewar dawo da shi ana saurara ta madaidaitan madaidaicin a cikin `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), wanda aka nuna anan don tunani - canza su yana buƙatar gyara lamba, ba env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` - tsawon lokacin da taga SSE ta buɗe don a iya sake gwadawa da wuri kafin kowane byte ya sadaukar ga abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` - hula mai wuya akan taga da aka riƙe; aikata (flush + passthrough) da zaran wannan da yawa bytes sun taru, ba tare da la'akari da mai ƙidayar lokaci ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` - max a bayyane ya sake buɗe rafi na sama yayin da riƙon baya har yanzu ba a cika shi ba.

> **Iyakar ƙimar tagar kowane mai bayarwa (babu env var):** FCC-ported ga kowane mai ba da sliding-window-limit _fallback_ yana wanzuwa a lambar (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, waya ta `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma jiragen ruwa tare da** fanko tsoho taswira** kuma yana da** babu mai aiki env var** yau - ana kunna shi ta hanyar ƙugiya / gyaran lamba kawai. Ba a jera shi da gangan ba a cikin teburin da ke sama. Matsakaicin gudun ba da sanda na per-`(token, IP)` wanda _yi_ yana da ƙulli shine `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

---

## 22. Debugging

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan masu canji suna samarwa** fitowar magana** kuma yana iya zubar da mahimman bayanai.** Kar a taɓa kunna samarwa.**

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                           | Fayil na asali                                                                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(Ba a saita ba)_                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna rajistan ayyukan zartarwar Cursor na verbose (wanda aka yanke chunks SSE, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Laƙabin `CURSOR_DEBUG` mai jituwa da baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(Ba a saita ba)_                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Hanyar fayil ɗin zaɓi wanda ke karɓar ɗanɗanan ɓangarorin Cursor da aka yanke lokacin `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                           | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Watsawa mara amfani (ms) ga mai aiwatar da Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                             | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tagar Grace (ms) bayan mawaƙin kv_after_text mai laushi mai laushi lokacin da bytes ya rage - yana ba da lokacin kiran kayan aiki na exec_mcp don kammala firam ɗin sa.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | kunna (`!== "0"`)                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Umurnin ƙaddamar da kayan aiki wanda ke sa mawaki-2.5 ya fitar da ingantaccen kiran kayan aiki. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## 23. GitHub Hadin kai

Bada masu amfani don ba da rahoton al'amura kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Mai canzawa           | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`        | Wurin ajiya a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`        | GitHub Alamar Samun Keɓaɓɓu tare da iyakar `issues:write`.                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | fitowar masu taimako / wakilin wakilin girgije | Gwargwadon damar samun damar GitHub da aka yi amfani da shi azaman koma baya ga `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` kuma masu taimakawa wakilin girgije ke cinyewa a cikin `src/lib/cloudAgent/*`. |

## Yanayin ƙaddamarwa

Don jagoranci na baya baya SRE (halayyar ts/bifrost/auto, 9router vs CLIProxyAPI sanyawa, da babban dabarun faɗuwar baya), duba [Dabarun Bayarwa Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Karamin Ci gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker Production

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Air-Gapped / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Kwarewar Sandbox (v3.8.0 +)

Iyakoki da ƙwanƙolin aminci da ake amfani da su lokacin da Tsarin Ƙwarewa (`src/lib/skills/`) yana aiwatar da ƙayyadaddun kayan aiki na mai amfani a cikin mahalli mai yashi.

| Mai canzawa                       | Tsohuwar                                | Fayil na asali                                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                          | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci na bangon-agogon aiki don lambar fasaha mai yashi. Ƙarfin wuya; wani abu ya fi tsayi a kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(ya koma `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwaƙwalwa Saita sama fiye da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da izinin tafiyar matakai masu yawa.                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da fasaha za ta iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya mai yashi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da aka ɗauka daga kowane amsa HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin fasaha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ƙarfin wuya a kan haruffan stdout/stderr sun dawo daga kiran akwatin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar hanyar sadarwa mai fita daga cikin akwatin yashi. Matsalolin zuwa** ware** domin aminci.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(Ba komai)_                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin izinin waƙafi-wakafi na hotunan kwantena da aka halatta don aiwatar da akwatin yashi. Babu komai yana nufin ginannen tsoho kawai.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(wanda aka gina a ciki)_               | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Hoton kwantena da aka yi amfani da shi lokacin zubar da akwatin yashi mai goyon bayan Docker. Juye don saka hoton tushe na al'ada.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                  | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Lokacin aikin kwantena don gwaninta sandboxing: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar mafi kyawun lokacin aikin da aka shigar kowane mai masaukin OS (Apple Container/OrbStack akan macOS, kwantena WSL akan Windows, Podman akan Linux), komawa zuwa Docker. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ƙaddamar da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fita daga lambar fasaha ta sabani. Haɗa tare da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da ƙaƙƙarfan manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a cikin turawa da aka raba.

Wuraren ƙarshen keɓaɓɓen mai ba da sabis, hanyoyin hanyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), 1Proxy egress pool, bayanan adana bayanai da ƙananan kowane nau'i ya soke abin da mai zartarwa Layer ko rubutun ya ambata.

| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | igiyar haɗin Redis don madaidaicin ƙimar baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(Mai watsa shiri)_                                                         | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Soke mai masaukin baki da aka yi amfani da shi don ɗauko jigogi na tsarin ƙididdigewa Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | samu daga mai masaukin baki                                                 | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Cikakkun adadin URL soke ga Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Karɓar laƙabi ga `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (ƙimar `ts \| bifrost \| auto` iri ɗaya). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` yana ɗaukar fifiko lokacin da aka saita duka biyun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Yana aiki kawai lokacin da `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanyar zuwa takardar shedar TLS PEM don bautar `omniroute serve` akan HTTPS (daidai da `--tls-cert`). | `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanya zuwa maɓallin keɓaɓɓen PEM TLS don `omniroute serve` HTTPS (daidai da `--tls-key`).
