### Claude Warmup Scheduler

Dumi-dumin da aka yi amfani da shi don haɗa haɗin Anthropic OAuth, don haka ana buɗe taga iyakar ƙimar awoyi 5 ta wani ƙaramin tsari da aka tsara maimakon ta ainihin ta farko (#8848). An kashe mai tsara jadawalin sai dai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` gaskiya ba ne** kuma** ana alamar haɗin haɗin a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; Jerin haɗin da babu komai yana nufin babu abin da aka ɗumama koda tare da env var a kunne.

| Mai canzawa                    | Tsohuwar                    | Fayil na asali               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(a kashe → )_              | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban canji don mai tsara dumama. Yana yarda da `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (wanda ba shi da hankali, datsa). Duk wata ƙima, ko rashin saitawa, tana barin mai tsarawa.        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Maganar cron-fili biyar don kaska mai dumi, wanda aka kimanta a `America/Los_Angeles` (sake saitin lokaci na Anthropic) ba tare da la'akari da agogon mai watsa shiri ba. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nawa haɗin haɗin gwiwa yana dumama daidai da kowane kaska. Maƙewa zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba ta ƙididdigewa ba ta koma `3`.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da aka yi amfani da shi don buƙatun dumama. Soke kawai idan babu tsoho akan shirin ku; zaɓi samfurin mafi arha wanda har yanzu yana buɗe taga.                   |

### Browser-Login VNC Sessions & Data-Dir Alias

Chromium +VNC mai kunshe da aka yi amfani da shi don ma'amala mai ma'amala da mai bincike-shigo da shaidar shaidar shiga (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da sunan `DATA_DIR` na gado. Duk zaɓin zaɓi - abubuwan da suka dace VNC sun yi niyya ga hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗe kuma an shafe su kawai don hoton kwantena na al'ada, tashar jiragen ruwa, ko daidaita yanayin rayuwa.

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                                                                 | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | alamar hoton Docker don kwandon shiga Chromium +VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko nuna wannan a hoto na al'ada.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binary na lokacin aikin kwantena da aka yi amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwandon VNC (misali saita zuwa `podman`).                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | tashar VNC/noVNC ta fallasa cikin kwantena.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol tashar jiragen ruwa a cikin akwati.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar bayanin martabar Chromium a cikin akwati.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Jagorar mai watsa shiri yana riƙe da ci gaba da bayanan bayanan shiga mai lilo.                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kashe lokacin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC mara aiki.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsa mai wuya (ms) akan zaman VNC guda ɗaya na rayuwa.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC na lokaci ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokaci ya ƙare (ms) yana jiran mai binciken kwantena ya zama shirye-shiryen CDP.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokaci ya ƙare (ms) don girbin session/cookies da aka kama bayan an gama shiga.                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni sun wuce zuwa Chromium mai kwantena.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙaddamar da hanyar sadarwa Docker kwandon shiga VNC yana haɗuwa (#12571) maimakon tsohuwar gada, don haka kwantena na 'yan'uwa ba za su iya isa tashar tashar CDP ta gada ba.                                |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Legacy alias** don `DATA_DIR`, dubawa ne kawai bayan duka `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ba a saita su ba. Yana gano ma'ajin zaman Notion -zaren yanar gizo (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & E2E Harness

Ana amfani da `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Bar kowace ƙima a ƙasa mara tushe a cikin jigilar samarwa.

| Mai canzawa                            | Tsohuwar                       | Fayil na asali                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin bootstrap E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | ya koma `INITIAL_PASSWORD`     | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Admin kalmar sirri allurar cikin yanayin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe zaben duba lafiyar gida yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madauki alamar lafiyar OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | An keɓe masu samar da waƙafi daga aikin share fage-sake (misali `codex,openai`). Zaɓin da aka yi niyya don cikakken kashe gwajin lafiya - gajerun masu samar da TTL suna ci gaba da wartsakewa yayin da masu samar da cascade ke zama mai amsawa-kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Shiru lafiya hayaniyar duba a cikin Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samar da Next.js kafin fara Playwright ( inganta CI ).                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                            | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake ƙugiya uninstall OmniRoute (wanda CI ke amfani dashi don kiyaye `node_modules` cikakke).                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                       | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Jiran lokaci (ms) don uwar garken ya zama lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login` | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL abin hawan hayaki na Electron yana tsammanin kunshin app ɗin zai yi aiki.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimlar ƙarewar lokaci (ms) kafin kayan aikin hayaki ya daina.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Sanya taga (ms) bayan loda shafin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(moto)_                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Bayyanar hanya zuwa kunshin Electron aiwatarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                     | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudun hayaƙin Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` don adana bayanan hayaƙi bayan an gama.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` don jera rajistan ayyukan Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudu.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake farawa da direktan bayanai iri ɗaya kuma tabbatar da ƙaddamarwa na biyu ya zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(PATH lookup)_                | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Mayar da hanyar binary Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Bututun fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run` ). Duk masu canji shida ba a saita su ta tsohuwa - saita su a cikin `.env` kawai akan injinan da yakamata su iya tafiyar da fassarar docs.

| Mai canzawa                                | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Buɗe tushen URL mai dacewa da AI don ƙarshen fassarar.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Alamar mai ɗauka don ƙarshen fassarar (ba a taɓa shiga ba).                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Model id, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Sarkar ƙirar waƙafi na zaɓi wanda aka gwada daidai lokacin da ƙirar farko ta kasa (ƙididdigar 429, 401/402, 5xx, ƙarewar lokaci, cibiyar sadarwa); ba a sake gwada buƙatun da ba daidai ba (400/413/422) a wani wuri. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci-lokacin-buƙata a cikin milli seconds.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Ikon tunani na zaɓi, aika kamar yadda yake zuwa ga baya lokacin da aka saita.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Buƙatun fassarar layi ɗaya lokacin gudana akan fayiloli / wurare da yawa.                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`             | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | `llm` yana amfani da ƙarshen baya a sama; `google` yana fassara ta hanyar injin Google mai aminci kuma babu buƙatar ko ɗaya daga cikin masu canjin LLM.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Tare da injin `llm`, gunkin samfurin ba zai iya samarwa ba (magadin fitarwa ya gaza akan kowane yunƙuri, ko kuskuren samfuri) ana fassara shi ta hanyar Google maimakon gazawar wurin. `0` yana kashe shi.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Maɓalli na Fassarar Cloud v2 na zaɓi. Unset yana amfani da mabuɗin ƙarshen gidan yanar gizo mara maɓalli (iyakantaccen ƙima, mai kyau don raƙuman ruwa lokaci-lokaci).                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`               | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Daidaitaccen buƙatun Google wanda kowane yanki na gudu ya raba.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`           | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci na kowane buƙatar injin Google, a cikin millise seconds.                                                                                                                                           |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Tallafin Kai)

Ƙarin zaɓi na zaɓi da alamar fasalin RADAR_ENABLED (tsoho a kashe - tutar fasalin da aka kunna ta Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Matsaloli huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa ƙetare na zaɓi na zaɓi don ciyarwa mai ɗaukar nauyi ko cokali mai yatsa da maɓalli mai goyan baya. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, keɓantaccen hanyar haɗin yanar gizo ce ta kyauta zuwa rukunin ayyukan sirri na mai shi. Dubi [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken tsarin doc da [kunnawar sa na ƙarshe-zuwa-ƙarshen da tsarin saitin jagora](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita mai karanta shelar Home/Changelog gabaɗaya ta hanyar canjin yanayi kuma baya dogara da alamar fasalin RADAR_ENABLED. Yana karanta ma'ajiyar jama'a `news.json` URL da aka ayyana a cikin `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta GET kawai; sallamar IDs ya kasance a cikin ma'ajiyar gida mai lilo.

| Mai canzawa                   | Tsohuwar                                     | Fayil na asali                                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Tushen URL wanda aka raba ta keɓaɓɓen kasidar sa hannu, masu ba da shawara, tayin tallafi, da ciyarwar Intel. Shake don nunawa a sabis mai ɗaukar nauyi ko cokali mai yatsa. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(maɓallin tsoho mai haɗawa)_                | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 maɓalli na jama'a (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) ana amfani dashi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwar ta al'ada.                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL maɓallin dashboard ɗin "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yana buɗewa (GitHub OAuth maɓallin da'awar maɓalli).                                                                  |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL maɓallin dashboard ɗin "Tallafawa aikin" yana buɗewa ( shafin payment/plans ).                                                                                           |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Mallakar ayyuka masu zaman kansu kawai-hanyar hanyar haɗin gwiwa. ana buƙatar HTTPS ban da madauki na HTTP SSH gaba; unset ko mara inganci ƙirƙira babu abin kewayawa.       |

## Audit: Cire / Matattu Canje-canje

Masu canji masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigar baya ta `.env.example` amma suna da** babu runtime references** a cikin codebase na yanzu. An cire su:

| Mai canzawa                                                                                                                                                                     | Dalili                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Kada a taɓa karanta ta kowane fayil na tushe. SQLite shine kawai direban da ke goyan baya - babu zaɓi da ake buƙata.                                            |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Gabatar da tsoffin samfuran docs/env amma ba a yi amfani da su ba a lokacin aiki. Maiyuwa zai dawo a cikin sifa mai nau'i-nau'i na gaba.                        |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a magana a cikin lambar tushe. Girman ma'ajin bayanai ba shi da iyaka ta hanyar wucin gadi.                                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a magana a cikin lambar tushe. An yi yuwuwa an cire gogewar gado.                                                                                            |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba binary CLI ).                                                    |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a magana a ko'ina a cikin lambar tushe.                                                                                                                      |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Lokacin gudu baya karanta waɗannan env vars - takaddun shaida sun fito daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rufaffen DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` - jujjuyawar sa ba ta da wani tasiri.                                                 |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran maɓallan maɓalli API da ba a amfani da su a cikin v3.8.0.                                                                            |

### Tsohuwar Ƙimar Gyara

| Mai canzawa               | Tsohuwar darajar `.env.example` | Ainihin Code Default | Kafaffen                                           |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                            | `7`                  | ✅ Cire ƙima mai ɓarna; an rubuta `7` azaman tsoho |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                            | `7`                  | ✅ Cire ƙima mai ɓarna; an rubuta `7` azaman tsoho |

### sake fasalin tsarin OpenCode (ad-hoc kayan aiki)

Ana amfani da `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sabunta `opencode.json` tare da madaidaitan ma'aunin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka ja daga misalin OmniRoute mai gudana. Babu ɗayan waɗannan da ake buƙata don aiki na yau da kullun - rubutun kayan aikin haɓaka ne kawai.

| Mai canzawa        | Tsohuwar                 | Fayil na asali                            | Bayani                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Tushen URL na misalin OmniRoute don tambayar `/v1/models`.                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | maɓallin API don tabbatarwa akan ƙarshen `/v1/models` OmniRoute Faɗuwa zuwa `OPENCODE_API_KEY` lokacin da ba a saita ba.    |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Buɗe-style API key (`sk-...`) rubuta a cikin sabunta `opencode.json`. Faɗuwa zuwa `OMNIROUTE_KEY` lokacin da ba a saita ba. |

### Matsawa layi-eval kayan aiki (ad-hoc kayan aiki)

Ana amfani da `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, ƙimar matsi ta layi CLI. Ba a buƙata don aiki na yau da kullun - kayan aikin haɓakawa kawai.

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar        | Fayil na asali                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (ba komai) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Takaddun shaidar JSON da aka kawo mai aiki don mai bayarwa wanda aka yi amfani da shi ta hanyar matsawa-eval CLI na layi (wanda aka kwatanta da `JSON.parse`). Bar unset don bushe gudu. |

### VNC Zama Mai Bidiyo

Ana amfani da `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium mara kai na tushen Docker don masu samar da injin bincike. Duk na zaɓi - tsoho wanda aka nuna a ƙasa.

| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | hoton Docker don kwandon Chromium VNC.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(tutocin da aka gina a ciki)_ | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin Chromium CLI args ya wuce zuwa mai binciken da ke cikin akwati.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | tashar VNC cikin akwati.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol tashar jiragen ruwa a cikin akwati.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Littafin bayanin martaba a cikin akwati.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Jagorar-gefen mai watsa shiri don bayanan martabar burauza masu tsayi.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kashe lokacin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC.                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin lokacin zama (ms).                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC na lokaci guda.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin shirye-shiryen Browser (ms).                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | lokacin Harvest/cleanup (ms).                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙaddamar da hanyar sadarwar Docker kwandon ya haɗa (#12571), a kashe tsohuwar gada. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Littafin jagora don jimrewar zaman zaren Notion.                                    |

## 24. Saki ƙarin v3.8.50

An gabatar da waɗannan saitunan bayan hoton kwangilar muhalli na baya.

| Mai canzawa                                 | Tsohuwar                               | Fayil na asali                                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Matsakaicin jiran ramin shigar da hira mai nauyi kafin `503` mai sake gwadawa; wani ɗan gajeren iyaka jira serializes wakili fashe maimakon `503` nan take. `0` yana dawo da kin amincewa nan take.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Kasafin kudin-bytes na jiran shiga: iyakoki jimillar tayoyin jiki da aka yi fakin faki-fadi don haka jira ba zai iya haɓaka tarin ba (#4380). Jiran sama da kasafin kuɗi suna karɓar `503` da za a sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60s)                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ƙaddamar da no-op tun #10110: an cire hanyoyin shigar da kowane zama a madadin tsarin kasafin kuɗi guda ɗaya. An karɓa don daidaitawa; watsi.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ƙaddamar da no-op tun #10110: an cire hanyoyin shigar da kowane zama a madadin tsarin kasafin kuɗi guda ɗaya. An karɓa don daidaitawa; watsi.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Bambance-bambance daga vars na kowane hanyar haɗin kai da ke sama (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, no-ops tun #10110). Dashboard alama tuta mai suna iri ɗaya; env var yayi nasara a kan dashboard override; yana buƙatar sake farawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Yana da iyaka tsawon lokacin da kiran gudu-yanzu ke jiran aikin cikin jirgin kafin a fara jerin gwano.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | kunna                                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana kiyaye IMS da jihar-hadarin burauza sabo ta hanyar zaman CDP na Chrome-asusu; saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | kunna                                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ya dage ya gyara zaman Adobe karkashin `DATA_DIR`; saita `0` don yanayin ƙwaƙwalwa-kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ƙarin kwanciyar hankali bayan kowane na uku na nasarar ƙaddamar da Adobe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `CHROME_PATH`                               | auto-ganewa                            | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Zabin cikakken aiwatar da Chrome na zaɓi wanda masu aiwatar da bincike ke amfani da shi lokacin gano atomatik na dandamali bai isa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(na ciki)_                            | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Mai sarrafa siginar uwar garken tsaye-tsaye; ana saitawa da sharewa ta mai ƙaddamarwa maimakon ma'aikatan da suka tsara su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `UI_KIT_CLONE`                              | `_references/cheaper-inference-ui-kit` | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-snapshot.mjs`                                                | Hanyar CI UI Kit ɗin keɓaɓɓen ƙofa mai ɗaukar hoto ta bambanta `packages/ui-kit/src` da (KEA-7886); Ƙofar ta tsallake tare da gargadi lokacin da clone ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### ChatGPT gizo (Codex)

Globale Defaults für den mara kai Browser da kuma ausgehenden Tool-Tunnel. Ni Dashboard yana haɓaka Haɗin-Werte haben Vorrang.

| Mai canzawa                             | Tsohuwar                       | Fayil na asali                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(Gano kai tsaye)_             | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Expliziter Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad für npm-, systemd- und PM2-Betrieb.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(Gano kai tsaye)_             | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Gemeinsamer Fallback don ƙarin bayani game da Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | CDP-Endpunkt na ciki; Docker verwendet den Sidecar auf Port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Saita zuwa `1` (tare da saita ƙarshen CDP ) don sake amfani da abin sarrafawa daga waje, sa hannun bayanan martabar burauza ta data kasance shafin ChatGPT maimakon keɓaɓɓen mahallin kowane juyi; Hakanan yana rufe shafukan ChatGPT na lokaci ɗaya a 1. Kashe ta tsohuwa. Dubi [bayanin martaba CDP mai tsayi](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Wenn gesetzt, muss jede Anfrage da CDP-Proxy-Sidecar diesen Wert im Header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` mitschicken (#13679). Ohne Wert leitet der Proxy unauthentifiziert weiter — dann schützt nur die Netzisolierung des Compose-Netzes `chatgpt-web-codex-net`. Erzeugen mit `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                             |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globale OpenAI-Tunnel-ID don shigar da Codex-Tool-Runden.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Maɓallin Lokaci na Ramin Duniya; Niemals in Logs ausgeben.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Sunan Exakter yana ba da damar yin amfani da ChatGPT-Custom-Connectors don mutuwa MCP-Brücke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Dediziertes Verzeichnis für Browser-, Broker- da Tunnelzustand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Yi `1` da Browser-Diagnosebilder da ƙwanƙwasa wuraren bincike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Zabi absolutter Pfad zu einem dauerhaften Launcher-Binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(Gano kai tsaye)_             | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Zabi Absoluter Pfad zum Bun-Runtime-Binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Legacy-Fallback für `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; neue Setups verwenden kanonischen Namen.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

## OmniConductor Bridge

Mabukacin SSE mai tsayi wanda ke nuna ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa cikin A2A TaskManager na gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ficewa - gadar tana farawa ne kawai lokacin da aka saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Gefen uwar garken kawai: alamar cibiya ba dole ba ne ta isa mai lilo.

| Mai canzawa                    | Tsohuwar                | Fayil na asali                   | Bayani                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Tushen URL na cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). An kashe gadar =.                                  |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Takaddun shaida don ciyarwar SSE - fitar da `spokesperson` -kind takwarorinsu akan cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).    |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Takaddun shaida don inbound A2A→ tawagar ɗawainiya (`POST /v1/tasks`); ya koma `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` idan ba a saita ba. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Tushen URL na sabis na mai magana da yawun (Faro) a bayan dashboard chat proxy (`/api/conductor/ask`).                   |

### Jadawalin ƙididdiga-sani

Ana amfani da `open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` don duba kasafin kuɗi na alamar kafin buƙatun. Ficewa - Tsohuwar dabi'ar tuƙi ba ta canzawa lokacin da ba a saita ba.

| Mai canzawa                        | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`      | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Lokacin da `1`, tsallake hanyoyin haɗin gwiwa waɗanda kasafin kuɗin tagar kowane-taga (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebur `provider_quota_state`) ba za su iya biyan ƙimar da aka ƙiyasta kafin aikawa ba. Kasa-bude lokacin da ba a saita kasafin kuɗi ba.                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(na)_   | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Gyaran martani na ƙididdige alamar tsaro na mahallin (#14931): kowane (mai bayarwa, samfuri, kayan aikin da ake bayarwa) EMA na `usage.prompt_tokens` / kimanta, manne zuwa 0.15-2.0, a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya kawai. `off`, `0` ko `false` yana hana yin rikodi da aikace-aikace. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`  | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Yi rijistar `xai-oauth` (`xao`) da hanyoyin hoton `grok-cli`. Dashboard alama tuta mai suna iri ɗaya; wani DB yayi nasara akan wannan darajar.                                                                                                                                     |
